"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health read model.

Provides a presentation-independent operational read model
for provider health information.

Architecture:

Provider Health Observation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        v
Provider Health Read Model
        |
        +-------------------+
        |                   |
        v                   v
Operations Center       Future Agent
Dashboard              Operations
        |                   |
        +---------+---------+
                  |
                  v
          Operational Intelligence


Responsibilities:

- expose provider health information in a dashboard-safe
  read model;
- retain provider/model/capability attribution;
- expose current health state;
- expose failure and recovery timing information;
- remain read-only.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- perform health probes;
- change Runtime routing;
- create incidents;
- send emails;
- approve recommendations;
- perform autonomous failover.

This module is an operational read model, not a second
health-management engine.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthState,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthReadModel:
    """
    Dashboard-safe provider health representation.

    Model and capability attribution are retained from the
    latest provider health observation while operational
    health state comes from ProviderHealthService.
    """

    provider: str

    model: str | None

    capability: str | None

    status: str

    checked_at: datetime

    consecutive_failures: int

    consecutive_successes: int

    last_success_at: datetime | None

    last_failure_at: datetime | None

    degraded_since: datetime | None

    unavailable_since: datetime | None

    active: bool = True


class ProviderHealthReadModelService:
    """
    Builds read-only provider health projections.

    This service does not calculate provider health itself.
    Health state remains owned by ProviderHealthService.
    """

    def build(
        self,
        *,
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
        state: ProviderHealthState,
    ) -> ProviderHealthReadModel:
        """
        Build a provider health read model from the latest
        observation and calculated health state.
        """

        if observation is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health observation cannot be None."
            )

        if state is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health state cannot be None."
            )

        if (
            observation.provider
            != state.provider
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Observation provider and health state "
                "provider must match."
            )

        return ProviderHealthReadModel(
            provider=state.provider,
            model=observation.model,
            capability=observation.capability,
            status=state.status.value,
            checked_at=state.checked_at,
            consecutive_failures=(
                state.consecutive_failures
            ),
            consecutive_successes=(
                state.consecutive_successes
            ),
            last_success_at=(
                state.last_success_at
            ),
            last_failure_at=(
                state.last_failure_at
            ),
            degraded_since=(
                state.degraded_since
            ),
            unavailable_since=(
                state.unavailable_since
            ),
            active=True,
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health service.

Transforms provider health observations into the current
provider health state and significant health transitions.

Architecture:

Provider Health Probe
        |
        v
ProviderHealthObservation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        +--------------------+
        |                    |
        v                    v
ProviderHealthState   HealthTransitionEvent
        |                    |
        +---------+----------+
                  |
                  v
        ProviderIncidentService
                  |
                  v
           EscalationService


Responsibilities:

- process provider health observations;
- maintain provider health state;
- track consecutive failures;
- track consecutive successes;
- detect degradation;
- detect unavailability;
- detect recovery;
- expose the current provider health state.

Does NOT:

- execute health probes;
- persist incidents;
- send emails;
- modify Runtime routing;
- perform autonomous failover.

The service is an application-layer health state
processor. Incident persistence and escalation remain
separate responsibilities.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthState,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
    ProviderHealthTransitionEvent,
)


class ProviderHealthService:
    """
    Application service responsible for provider health
    state tracking.

    Health policy for the Hackathon:

    1. First failure:
       DEGRADED

    2. Three consecutive failures:
       UNAVAILABLE

    3. Successful observation after degradation:
       HEALTHY

    4. Successful observation after unavailability:
       HEALTHY / RECOVERED transition

    The thresholds are intentionally kept in the service
    for the Hackathon and can later become validated
    configuration.
    """

    DEGRADED_FAILURE_THRESHOLD = 1

    UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD = 3

    RECOVERY_SUCCESS_THRESHOLD = 2

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Initialise an empty provider health registry.
        """

        self._states: dict[
            str,
            ProviderHealthState,
        ] = {}

    def observe(
        self,
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
    ) -> ProviderHealthTransitionEvent:
        """
        Process one provider health observation.

        Returns the health transition generated by the
        observation.

        The method updates only application-level health
        state. It does not create incidents or external
        side effects.
        """

        if observation is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health observation cannot be None."
            )

        self._validate_observation(
            observation
        )

        previous_state = self._states.get(
            observation.provider
        )

        next_state = self._calculate_next_state(
            observation=observation,
            previous_state=previous_state,
        )

        self._states[
            observation.provider
        ] = next_state

        transition = (
            self._determine_transition(
                previous_state=previous_state,
                current_state=next_state,
            )
        )

        return ProviderHealthTransitionEvent(
            provider=observation.provider,
            transition=transition,
            previous_status=(
                previous_state.status
                if previous_state is not None
                else ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN
            ),
            current_status=next_state.status,
            occurred_at=observation.observed_at,
            consecutive_failures=(
                next_state.consecutive_failures
            ),
            consecutive_successes=(
                next_state.consecutive_successes
            ),
            affected_capabilities=(
                (observation.capability,)
                if observation.capability
                else ()
            ),
        )

    def get_state(
        self,
        provider: str,
    ) -> ProviderHealthState | None:
        """
        Return the current state of a provider.

        Returns None when the provider has not yet been
        observed.
        """

        if not provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider cannot be empty."
            )

        return self._states.get(
            provider
        )

    def get_all_states(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[ProviderHealthState, ...]:
        """
        Return current health states for all observed
        providers.
        """

        return tuple(
            self._states[
                provider
            ]
            for provider in sorted(
                self._states
            )
        )

    def clear(
        self,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Clear tracked health state.

        Intended primarily for tests and isolated
        demonstrations.
        """

        self._states.clear()

    def _calculate_next_state(
        self,
        *,
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
        previous_state: ProviderHealthState | None,
    ) -> ProviderHealthState:
        """
        Calculate the next provider health state.
        """

        if observation.status == (
            ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
        ):
            consecutive_failures = (
                observation.consecutive_failures
                if observation.consecutive_failures > 0
                else (
                    (
                        previous_state.consecutive_failures
                        if previous_state is not None
                        else 0
                    )
                    + 1
                )
            )

            return ProviderHealthState(
                provider=observation.provider,
                status=(
                    ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
                    if consecutive_failures
                    >= self.UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
                    else ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
                ),
                checked_at=observation.observed_at,
                consecutive_failures=(
                    consecutive_failures
                ),
                consecutive_successes=0,
                last_success_at=(
                    previous_state.last_success_at
                    if previous_state is not None
                    else None
                ),
                last_failure_at=(
                    observation.observed_at
                ),
                degraded_since=(
                    previous_state.degraded_since
                    if (
                        previous_state is not None
                        and previous_state.degraded_since
                        is not None
                    )
                    else observation.observed_at
                ),
                unavailable_since=(
                    (
                        previous_state.unavailable_since
                        if previous_state is not None
                        and previous_state.unavailable_since
                        is not None
                        else observation.observed_at
                    )
                    if consecutive_failures
                    >= self.UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
                    else None
                ),
            )

        if observation.status == (
            ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
        ):
            consecutive_failures = (
                observation.consecutive_failures
                if observation.consecutive_failures > 0
                else (
                    (
                        previous_state.consecutive_failures
                        if previous_state is not None
                        else 0
                    )
                    + 1
                )
            )

            return ProviderHealthState(
                provider=observation.provider,
                status=(
                    ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
                    if consecutive_failures
                    >= self.UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
                    else ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
                ),
                checked_at=observation.observed_at,
                consecutive_failures=(
                    consecutive_failures
                ),
                consecutive_successes=0,
                last_success_at=(
                    previous_state.last_success_at
                    if previous_state is not None
                    else None
                ),
                last_failure_at=(
                    observation.observed_at
                ),
                degraded_since=(
                    previous_state.degraded_since
                    if (
                        previous_state is not None
                        and previous_state.degraded_since
                        is not None
                    )
                    else observation.observed_at
                ),
                unavailable_since=(
                    (
                        previous_state.unavailable_since
                        if previous_state is not None
                        and previous_state.unavailable_since
                        is not None
                        else observation.observed_at
                    )
                    if consecutive_failures
                    >= self.UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
                    else None
                ),
            )

        if observation.status == (
            ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
        ):
            consecutive_successes = (
                observation.consecutive_successes
                if observation.consecutive_successes > 0
                else (
                    (
                        previous_state.consecutive_successes
                        if previous_state is not None
                        else 0
                    )
                    + 1
                )
            )

            return ProviderHealthState(
                provider=observation.provider,
                status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
                checked_at=observation.observed_at,
                consecutive_failures=0,
                consecutive_successes=(
                    consecutive_successes
                ),
                last_success_at=(
                    observation.observed_at
                ),
                last_failure_at=(
                    previous_state.last_failure_at
                    if previous_state is not None
                    else None
                ),
                degraded_since=None,
                unavailable_since=None,
            )

        return ProviderHealthState(
            provider=observation.provider,
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN,
            checked_at=observation.observed_at,
            consecutive_failures=(
                previous_state.consecutive_failures
                if previous_state is not None
                else 0
            ),
            consecutive_successes=(
                previous_state.consecutive_successes
                if previous_state is not None
                else 0
            ),
            last_success_at=(
                previous_state.last_success_at
                if previous_state is not None
                else None
            ),
            last_failure_at=(
                previous_state.last_failure_at
                if previous_state is not None
                else None
            ),
            degraded_since=(
                previous_state.degraded_since
                if previous_state is not None
                else None
            ),
            unavailable_since=(
                previous_state.unavailable_since
                if previous_state is not None
                else None
            ),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _determine_transition(
        *,
        previous_state: ProviderHealthState | None,
        current_state: ProviderHealthState,
    ) -> ProviderHealthTransition:
        """
        Determine the significant transition represented
        by the latest observation.
        """

        previous_status = (
            previous_state.status
            if previous_state is not None
            else ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN
        )

        current_status = current_state.status

        if (
            previous_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
            and current_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
        ):
            return ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED

        if (
            current_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
        ):
            return ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE

        if (
            current_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
        ):
            return ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED

        if (
            current_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN
        ):
            return ProviderHealthTransition.UNKNOWN

        return ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_observation(
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate required health observation fields.
        """

        if not observation.provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider cannot be empty."
            )

        if observation.latency_ms is not None:
            if observation.latency_ms < 0:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Latency cannot be negative."
                )

        if observation.consecutive_failures < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "Consecutive failures cannot be negative."
            )

        if observation.consecutive_successes < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "Consecutive successes cannot be negative."
            )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\app\application\provider_management\provider_incident_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider incident service.

Tracks sustained provider availability incidents.

Architecture:

Provider Health Service
        |
        v
ProviderHealthTransitionEvent
        |
        v
ProviderIncidentService
        |
        +----------------------+
        |                      |
        v                      v
ProviderIncident       Incident Repository
        |                      |
        +----------+-----------+
                   |
                   +----------------------+
                   |                      |
                   v                      v
            Escalation Service   Operational Event Service
                                           |
                                           v
                                  Operations Center


Responsibilities:

- create provider incidents when a provider becomes
  unavailable;
- update open incidents;
- track outage start time;
- track latest observation;
- record affected capabilities;
- close incidents after recovery;
- calculate completed downtime;
- retrieve incident history;
- optionally publish operational lifecycle events.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- perform health probes;
- modify Runtime routing;
- send emails;
- approve recommendations;
- perform autonomous failover.

Incident persistence is kept behind a repository boundary.
The current implementation uses an in-memory repository
for Hackathon validation.

Operational event publication is optional so that existing
incident-service callers remain backwards compatible.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import uuid4

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
    ProviderHealthTransitionEvent,
    ProviderIncident,
)


class ProviderIncidentRepository:
    """
    Minimal persistence contract for provider incidents.

    A database-backed implementation can replace the
    in-memory implementation without changing the service.
    """

    def save(
        self,
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Persist an incident.
        """

        raise NotImplementedError

    def get_open(
        self,
        provider: str,
    ) -> ProviderIncident | None:
        """
        Retrieve the currently open incident for a provider.
        """

        raise NotImplementedError

    def get_all(
        self,
    ) -> list[ProviderIncident]:
        """
        Retrieve incident history.
        """

        raise NotImplementedError


class InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository(
    ProviderIncidentRepository,
):
    """
    In-memory incident repository.

    Intended for:

    - development;
    - testing;
    - Hackathon validation.

    It can later be replaced with SQLite or PostgreSQL.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._incidents: list[
            ProviderIncident
        ] = []

    def save(
        self,
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Save an incident.

        If the incident already exists, replace it.
        """

        if incident is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider incident cannot be None."
            )

        for index, existing in enumerate(
            self._incidents
        ):
            if (
                existing.incident_id
                == incident.incident_id
            ):
                self._incidents[index] = incident
                return

        self._incidents.append(
            incident
        )

    def get_open(
        self,
        provider: str,
    ) -> ProviderIncident | None:
        """
        Return the open incident for a provider.
        """

        for incident in reversed(
            self._incidents
        ):
            if (
                incident.provider == provider
                and incident.is_open
            ):
                return incident

        return None

    def get_all(
        self,
    ) -> list[ProviderIncident]:
        """
        Return incident history as a copy.
        """

        return list(
            self._incidents
        )


class ProviderIncidentService:
    """
    Application service responsible for provider
    availability incident lifecycle.

    Lifecycle:

        HEALTHY
           |
           v
        DEGRADED
           |
           v
        UNAVAILABLE
           |
           v
        OPEN INCIDENT
           |
           v
        RECOVERED
           |
           v
        CLOSED INCIDENT

    Operational events:

        New unavailable incident
                |
                v
        PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED

        Recovered open incident
                |
                v
        PROVIDER_RECOVERED
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: ProviderIncidentRepository,
        operational_event_service: (
            OperationalEventService | None
        ) = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the incident service.

        The operational event service is optional to preserve
        backwards compatibility with existing callers and tests.
        """

        if repository is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider incident repository "
                "cannot be None."
            )

        self._repository = repository

        self._operational_event_service = (
            operational_event_service
        )

    def process_transition(
        self,
        event: ProviderHealthTransitionEvent,
    ) -> ProviderIncident | None:
        """
        Process a provider health transition.

        Returns:

        - the newly created incident;
        - the updated incident;
        - the recovered incident;

        or None when no incident lifecycle action
        is required.

        The service does not send notifications or
        trigger provider changes.
        """

        if event is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health transition "
                "cannot be None."
            )

        if not event.provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider cannot be empty."
            )

        if (
            event.transition
            == ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE
        ):
            return self._handle_unavailable(
                event
            )

        if (
            event.transition
            == ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED
        ):
            return self._handle_recovery(
                event
            )

        if (
            event.transition
            == ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
        ):
            return self._handle_degraded(
                event
            )

        return None

    def list_incidents(
        self,
    ) -> list[ProviderIncident]:
        """
        Return provider incident history.
        """

        return self._repository.get_all()

    def get_open_incident(
        self,
        provider: str,
    ) -> ProviderIncident | None:
        """
        Return the currently open incident for a provider.
        """

        if not provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider cannot be empty."
            )

        return self._repository.get_open(
            provider
        )

    def _handle_unavailable(
        self,
        event: ProviderHealthTransitionEvent,
    ) -> ProviderIncident:
        """
        Create or update an unavailable-provider incident.

        A new incident emits PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED.

        Repeated unavailable observations update the existing
        incident but do not create duplicate lifecycle events.
        """

        existing = (
            self._repository.get_open(
                event.provider
            )
        )

        if existing is not None:
            updated = ProviderIncident(
                incident_id=existing.incident_id,
                provider=existing.provider,
                status=(
                    ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
                ),
                started_at=existing.started_at,
                last_observed_at=(
                    event.occurred_at
                ),
                recovered_at=None,
                affected_capabilities=(
                    self._merge_capabilities(
                        existing.affected_capabilities,
                        event.affected_capabilities,
                    )
                ),
                consecutive_failures=(
                    event.consecutive_failures
                ),
                resolution_message=None,
            )

            self._repository.save(
                updated
            )

            return updated

        incident = ProviderIncident(
            incident_id=str(uuid4()),
            provider=event.provider,
            status=(
                ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
            ),
            started_at=event.occurred_at,
            last_observed_at=event.occurred_at,
            recovered_at=None,
            affected_capabilities=(
                tuple(
                    sorted(
                        set(
                            event.affected_capabilities
                        )
                    )
                )
            ),
            consecutive_failures=(
                event.consecutive_failures
            ),
            resolution_message=None,
        )

        self._repository.save(
            incident
        )

        self._publish_incident_created(
            incident
        )

        return incident

    def _handle_recovery(
        self,
        event: ProviderHealthTransitionEvent,
    ) -> ProviderIncident | None:
        """
        Close the provider's open incident after recovery.

        A successfully closed incident emits
        PROVIDER_RECOVERED.
        """

        existing = (
            self._repository.get_open(
                event.provider
            )
        )

        if existing is None:
            return None

        recovered = ProviderIncident(
            incident_id=existing.incident_id,
            provider=existing.provider,
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            started_at=existing.started_at,
            last_observed_at=event.occurred_at,
            recovered_at=event.occurred_at,
            affected_capabilities=(
                self._merge_capabilities(
                    existing.affected_capabilities,
                    event.affected_capabilities,
                )
            ),
            consecutive_failures=(
                existing.consecutive_failures
            ),
            resolution_message=(
                "Provider recovered after "
                "successful health observations."
            ),
        )

        self._repository.save(
            recovered
        )

        self._publish_provider_recovered(
            recovered
        )

        return recovered

    def _handle_degraded(
        self,
        event: ProviderHealthTransitionEvent,
    ) -> ProviderIncident | None:
        """
        Update an existing incident if the provider
        is already under an open incident.

        A single degraded observation does not create
        a provider outage incident.
        """

        existing = (
            self._repository.get_open(
                event.provider
            )
        )

        if existing is None:
            return None

        updated = ProviderIncident(
            incident_id=existing.incident_id,
            provider=existing.provider,
            status=existing.status,
            started_at=existing.started_at,
            last_observed_at=event.occurred_at,
            recovered_at=None,
            affected_capabilities=(
                self._merge_capabilities(
                    existing.affected_capabilities,
                    event.affected_capabilities,
                )
            ),
            consecutive_failures=(
                event.consecutive_failures
            ),
            resolution_message=None,
        )

        self._repository.save(
            updated
        )

        return updated

    def _publish_incident_created(
        self,
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Publish a provider incident-created operational event
        when the integration service is configured.
        """

        if (
            self._operational_event_service
            is None
        ):
            return

        event = (
            self._operational_event_service
            .record_incident_created(
                incident=incident,
            )
        )

        if event is None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Provider incident operational "
                "event service returned no event."
            )

    def _publish_provider_recovered(
        self,
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Publish a provider recovery operational event
        when the integration service is configured.
        """

        if (
            self._operational_event_service
            is None
        ):
            return

        event = (
            self._operational_event_service
            .record_provider_recovered(
                incident=incident,
            )
        )

        if event is None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Provider recovery operational "
                "event service returned no event."
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _merge_capabilities(
        existing: tuple[str, ...],
        incoming: tuple[str, ...],
    ) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        """
        Merge affected capabilities without duplicates.
        """

        return tuple(
            sorted(
                set(existing)
                | set(incoming)
            )
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\app\application\provider_management\provider_manager_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center Provider Manager service.

Coordinates provider intelligence for the Operations Center.

Architecture:

Provider Usage
        |
        v
Provider Comparison Analytics
        |
        v
Capability Optimisation
        |
        v
ProviderManagerService
        |
        +--------------------+
        |                    |
        v                    v
Recommendation          Escalation
        |                    |
        +---------+----------+
                  |
                  v
            Human Approval


Responsibilities:

- coordinate provider comparison analytics;
- generate capability optimisation recommendations;
- identify when a recommendation represents a
  meaningful operational change;
- provide a single application-level entry point
  for the Operations Center.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- modify Runtime routing;
- approve recommendations;
- send emails;
- autonomously change production configuration.

This service is an orchestration boundary for the
AI Operations Center.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.application.provider_management.capability_optimisation_service import (
    CapabilityOptimisationRecommendation,
    CapabilityOptimisationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_comparison_service import (
    ProviderComparison,
    ProviderComparisonService,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderManagerAnalysis:
    """
    Provider Manager analysis result.

    Contains the evidence and the resulting advisory
    recommendation.

    No operational action is performed.
    """

    capability: str

    comparisons: tuple[
        ProviderComparison,
        ...]

    recommendation: (
        CapabilityOptimisationRecommendation
    )


class ProviderManagerService:
    """
    Application orchestration service for the
    AI Service Provider Manager.

    The service coordinates existing application
    services instead of duplicating their logic.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        comparison_service: ProviderComparisonService,
        optimisation_service: (
            CapabilityOptimisationService
        ),
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the Provider Manager.

        Dependencies are injected to preserve:

        - testability;
        - dependency inversion;
        - provider independence.
        """

        if comparison_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Comparison service cannot be None."
            )

        if optimisation_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Optimisation service cannot be None."
            )

        self._comparison_service = (
            comparison_service
        )

        self._optimisation_service = (
            optimisation_service
        )

    def analyse(
        self,
        *,
        capability: str,
        current_provider: str,
        current_model: str,
        events: list,
    ) -> ProviderManagerAnalysis:
        """
        Analyse provider performance for a capability.

        Flow:

            Usage Events
                 |
                 v
            Comparison
                 |
                 v
            Optimisation
                 |
                 v
            Analysis

        The returned recommendation remains advisory.
        """

        if not capability.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Capability cannot be empty."
            )

        if not current_provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Current provider cannot be empty."
            )

        if not current_model.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Current model cannot be empty."
            )

        comparisons = (
            self._comparison_service
            .compare(
                events=events
            )
        )

        recommendation = (
            self._optimisation_service
            .recommend_from_comparisons(
                capability=capability,
                current_provider=(
                    current_provider
                ),
                current_model=current_model,
                comparisons=comparisons,
            )
        )

        capability_comparisons = tuple(
            comparison
            for comparison in comparisons
            if comparison.capability
            == capability
        )

        return ProviderManagerAnalysis(
            capability=capability,
            comparisons=(
                capability_comparisons
            ),
            recommendation=(
                recommendation
            ),
        )

------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider operational event service.

Provides the provider-management adapter between provider-specific
operational lifecycle facts and the generic OperationalEventService.

Architecture:

Provider Incident Service
        |
        v
ProviderOperationalEventService
        |
        v
OperationalEventService
        |
        v
OperationalEventRepository
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventSeverity,
    OperationalEventType,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderIncident,
)


class ProviderOperationalEventService:
    """
    Translate provider-management lifecycle facts into the
    generic operational-event contract.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        operational_event_service: OperationalEventService,
    ) -> None:
        if operational_event_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event service cannot be None."
            )

        self._operational_event_service = (
            operational_event_service
        )

    def record_incident_created(
        self,
        *,
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> OperationalEvent:
        """
        Record a provider incident-created operational fact.
        """

        if incident is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider incident cannot be None."
            )

        event = OperationalEvent.create(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED
            ),
            severity=OperationalEventSeverity.CRITICAL,
            source="provider_management",
            component="ProviderIncidentService",
            occurred_at=incident.started_at,
            message=(
                f"Provider incident created "
                f"for {incident.provider}."
            ),
            correlation_id=incident.incident_id,
            metadata={
                "provider": incident.provider,
                "incident_id": incident.incident_id,
                "status": incident.status.value,
                "affected_capabilities": list(
                    incident.affected_capabilities
                ),
                "consecutive_failures": (
                    incident.consecutive_failures
                ),
            },
        )

        return self._operational_event_service.record(
            event
        )

    def record_provider_recovered(
        self,
        *,
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> OperationalEvent:
        """
        Record a provider-recovered operational fact.
        """

        if incident is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider incident cannot be None."
            )

        if incident.recovered_at is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Recovered provider incident must "
                "have a recovery timestamp."
            )

        event = OperationalEvent.create(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_RECOVERED
            ),
            severity=OperationalEventSeverity.INFO,
            source="provider_management",
            component="ProviderIncidentService",
            occurred_at=incident.recovered_at,
            message=(
                f"Provider {incident.provider} "
                f"recovered."
            ),
            correlation_id=incident.incident_id,
            metadata={
                "provider": incident.provider,
                "incident_id": incident.incident_id,
                "status": incident.status.value,
                "affected_capabilities": list(
                    incident.affected_capabilities
                ),
                "downtime_seconds": (
                    incident.downtime_seconds
                ),
            },
        )

        return self._operational_event_service.record(
            event
        )

"""
AI Service Provider Manager recommendation service.

Generates provider optimisation recommendations.

Architecture:

Provider Analytics
        |
        v
Recommendation Service
        |
        v
ProviderRecommendation


Responsibilities:

- analyse provider options;
- generate optimisation proposals;
- explain recommendation reasoning.


Does NOT:

- switch providers;
- modify Runtime configuration;
- execute AI calls;
- approve changes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    ProviderRecommendation,
    RecommendationStatus,
)


class ProviderRecommendationService:
    """
    Generates provider management recommendations.

    Current implementation:

    - deterministic;
    - rule based;
    - suitable for hackathon.

    Future:

    - AI reasoning agent;
    - historical optimisation;
    - adaptive capability policies.
    """

    def recommend_provider_change(
        self,
        *,
        capability: str,
        current_provider: str,
        current_model: str,
        recommended_provider: str,
        recommended_model: str,
        reason: str,
        confidence: float,
    ) -> ProviderRecommendation:
        """
        Create a provider change recommendation.

        The recommendation requires human review.
        """

        if not capability.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Capability cannot be empty."
            )

        if not current_provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Current provider cannot be empty."
            )

        if not recommended_provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Recommended provider cannot be empty."
            )

        if confidence < 0 or confidence > 100:
            raise ValueError(
                "Confidence must be between 0 and 100."
            )

        return ProviderRecommendation(
            capability=capability,
            current_provider=current_provider,
            current_model=current_model,
            recommended_provider=(
                recommended_provider
            ),
            recommended_model=recommended_model,
            reason=reason,
            confidence=confidence,
            status=(
                RecommendationStatus.REVIEW_REQUIRED
            ),
        )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center dashboard API adapter.

Provides a thin presentation/API boundary over the
Operations Center dashboard facade.

Architecture:

Provider Management Application
        |
        v
OperationsCenterDashboardFacade
        |
        v
OperationsCenterDashboardAPI
        |
        v
HTTP / Frontend


Responsibilities:

- expose the dashboard through a stable API-facing contract;
- validate API inputs;
- delegate dashboard generation to the application facade;
- return JSON-safe data.

Does NOT:

- access repositories directly;
- execute providers;
- modify Runtime routing;
- approve escalations;
- reject escalations;
- send emails;
- perform autonomous operational actions.

Business logic remains in the application layer.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_facade import (
    OperationsCenterDashboardFacade,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_service import (
    ActiveProvider,
)


class OperationsCenterDashboardAPI:
    """
    Thin API adapter for the Operations Center dashboard.

    This class deliberately contains no provider-management
    business rules.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        facade: OperationsCenterDashboardFacade,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the API adapter.
        """

        if facade is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operations Center dashboard facade "
                "cannot be None."
            )

        self._facade = facade

    def get_dashboard(
        self,
        *,
        active_providers: tuple[
            ActiveProvider,
            ...
        ],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Return the Operations Center dashboard.

        The API adapter delegates completely to the
        application facade.
        """

        if active_providers is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Active providers cannot be None."
            )

        return self._facade.get_dashboard(
            active_providers=active_providers,
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\app\interfaces\provider_management\operations_center_dashboard_router.py

"""EasyChatBot AI Operations Center HTTP router.

Exposes the read-only Operations Center dashboard and
operational-event history through the HTTP/API boundary.

HTTP contract:

    GET /api/operations-center/dashboard
    GET /api/operations-center/operational-events

The router only delegates to already-composed application APIs.
It does not access repositories, create events, execute providers,
modify routing, or perform autonomous actions.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_api import (
    OperationsCenterDashboardAPI,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_composition import (
    OperationsCenterComposition,
    create_operations_center_composition,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_operational_events_api import (
    OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI,
)

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/operations-center",
    tags=["AI Operations Center"],
)

# Compose the complete graph once so the dashboard and operational-event
# APIs belong to the same Operations Center composition boundary.
_composition: OperationsCenterComposition = (
    create_operations_center_composition()
)

_dashboard_api: OperationsCenterDashboardAPI = (
    _composition.dashboard_api
)

_operational_events_api: OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI = (
    _composition.operational_events_api
)


def configure_dashboard_api(
    dashboard_api: OperationsCenterDashboardAPI,
) -> None:
    """Replace the configured dashboard API."""
    if dashboard_api is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "Operations Center dashboard API cannot be None."
        )

    global _dashboard_api
    _dashboard_api = dashboard_api


def get_dashboard_api() -> OperationsCenterDashboardAPI:
    """Resolve the configured dashboard API."""
    return _dashboard_api


def configure_operational_events_api(
    operational_events_api: OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI,
) -> None:
    """Replace the configured operational-events API."""
    if operational_events_api is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "Operations Center operational events API cannot be None."
        )

    global _operational_events_api
    _operational_events_api = operational_events_api


def get_operational_events_api() -> OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI:
    """Resolve the configured operational-events API."""
    return _operational_events_api


@router.get("/dashboard")
def get_operations_center_dashboard(
    dashboard_api: OperationsCenterDashboardAPI = Depends(
        get_dashboard_api
    ),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return the current Operations Center dashboard."""
    return dashboard_api.get_dashboard()


@router.get("/operational-events")
def get_operations_center_operational_events(
    operational_events_api: OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI = Depends(
        get_operational_events_api
    ),
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return the current Operations Center operational-event history."""
    return operational_events_api.list_events()

"""
Provider boundary tests.

Validates:

Capability
    |
    v
Provider Contract
    |
    v
Provider Implementation


The tests ensure:

- Runtime remains provider independent;
- capability execution does not know provider SDKs;
- future Parallel/Gemini integrations can be added behind contracts.

No external APIs are called.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest


from src.ai_runtime.podcast.providers import (
    PodcastProvider,
)


class FakePodcastProvider:
    """
    Controlled provider implementation.
    """

    provider_id = "fake-provider"

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
    ) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
        return [
            {
                "title": "Fake Podcast",
                "source": self.provider_id,
                "query": query,
            }
        ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_provider_contract_returns_results():
    """
    Provider implementation must satisfy
    the Runtime provider boundary.
    """

    provider: PodcastProvider = (
        FakePodcastProvider()
    )

    results = await provider.search(
        "French learning",
    )

    assert len(results) == 1

    assert (
        results[0]["source"]
        == "fake-provider"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_provider_remains_independent_from_runtime():
    """
    Runtime provider contract should not
    require external SDK dependencies.
    """

    provider = FakePodcastProvider()

    result = await provider.search(
        "AI education",
    )

    assert result[0]["title"] == "Fake Podcast"

import json

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.apple import ApplePodcastProvider
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    LearningContext,
    PodcastProviderName,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
)
from src.ai_runtime.podcast.spotify import SpotifyPodcastProvider


class FakeResponse:
    def __init__(self, payload: dict):
        self.payload = payload

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb):
        return False

    def read(self):
        return json.dumps(self.payload).encode("utf-8")


def intent() -> PodcastSearchIntent:
    return PodcastSearchIntent(
        user_id="user-001",
        learning_context=LearningContext(
            world_id="French World",
            target_language="fr",
            learner_level="B2",
        ),
        topics=("artificial intelligence",),
    )


def test_apple_normalises_language(monkeypatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "src.ai_runtime.podcast.apple.urlopen",
        lambda *args, **kwargs: FakeResponse(
            {
                "results": [
                    {
                        "kind": "podcast",
                        "collectionId": 123,
                        "collectionName": "IA en France",
                        "artistName": "Demo Publisher",
                        "language": "French",
                        "collectionViewUrl": (
                            "https://podcasts.apple.com/example"
                        ),
                    }
                ]
            }
        ),
    )

    results = ApplePodcastProvider().search(intent())

    assert len(results) == 1
    assert results[0].provider == PodcastProviderName.APPLE
    assert results[0].languages == ("fr",)


def test_spotify_normalises_episode_language(monkeypatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "src.ai_runtime.podcast.spotify.urlopen",
        lambda *args, **kwargs: FakeResponse(
            {
                "episodes": {
                    "items": [
                        {
                            "id": "episode-001",
                            "name": "IA en Europe",
                            "description": "European AI regulation",
                            "languages": ["fr"],
                            "duration_ms": 1800000,
                            "release_date": "2026-08-10",
                            "external_urls": {
                                "spotify": (
                                    "https://open.spotify.com/episode/episode-001"
                                )
                            },
                            "images": [],
                        }
                    ]
                }
            }
        ),
    )

    results = SpotifyPodcastProvider(
        access_token="test-token"
    ).search(intent())

    assert len(results) == 1
    assert results[0].provider == PodcastProviderName.SPOTIFY
    assert results[0].languages == ("fr",)
    assert results[0].duration_seconds == 1800

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\ai_runtime\test_parallel_provider.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
Tests for Parallel Podcast Provider.

Validates:

PodcastDiscoveryExecutor
        |
        v
PodcastProvider Contract
        |
        v
ParallelPodcastProvider

These tests do not call:

- Parallel API
- external providers
- Gemini
- Google Cloud
"""


from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import Mock

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.models import (
    LearningContext,
    PodcastSearchIntent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.podcast.parallel import (
    ParallelPodcastProvider,
)


def create_test_intent() -> PodcastSearchIntent:
    """
    Create deterministic provider-neutral search intent.
    """

    return PodcastSearchIntent(
        user_id="test-user",
        learning_context=LearningContext(
            world_id="hackathon",
            target_language="fr",
            learner_level="B2",
        ),
        topics=(
            "French learning",
        ),
    )


def test_parallel_provider_without_client_returns_empty_results():
    """
    Sprint 0 behaviour.

    Without an injected client:

    - no external calls;
    - deterministic response.
    """

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider()

    results = provider.search(
        create_test_intent(),
    )

    assert results == []


def test_parallel_provider_exposes_provider_id():
    """
    Provider exposes stable identifier.
    """

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider()

    assert provider.provider_id == "parallel"


def test_parallel_provider_uses_injected_client():
    """
    Provider must use dependency injection.

    It must not construct external clients itself.
    """

    client = Mock()

    client.search.return_value = []

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider(
        client=client,
    )

    provider.search(
        create_test_intent(),
    )

    client.search.assert_called_once()


def test_parallel_provider_maps_results():
    """
    External provider responses must be converted
    into provider-neutral PodcastSearchResult objects.
    """

    client = Mock()

    client.search.return_value = [
        {
            "id": "episode-001",
            "title": "French Podcast",
            "description": "Learning French",
            "publisher": "Parallel",
            "languages": [
                "fr",
            ],
            "url": (
                "https://example.com/podcast"
            ),
        }
    ]

    provider = ParallelPodcastProvider(
        client=client,
    )

    results = provider.search(
        create_test_intent(),
    )

    assert len(results) == 1

    result = results[0]

    assert result.title == "French Podcast"
    assert result.publisher == "Parallel"
    assert result.url.startswith(
        "https://"
    )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\api\test_operations_center_http_bootstrap.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot Operations Center HTTP bootstrap tests.

Validates that the existing FastAPI application bootstrap
registers the Operations Center router.

This test intentionally verifies the existing application
bootstrap boundary. It does not create a second FastAPI app
or a second Runtime.
"""

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from backend.api.main import create_app


def test_operations_center_dashboard_route_is_registered() -> None:
    """
    The existing FastAPI application must expose the
    Operations Center dashboard endpoint.
    """

    app = create_app()

    client = TestClient(
        app,
        raise_server_exceptions=False,
    )

    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/dashboard"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert isinstance(
        body,
        dict,
    )


def test_operations_center_operational_events_route_is_registered() -> None:
    """
    The existing FastAPI application must expose the
    read-only operational-events endpoint.
    """

    app = create_app()

    client = TestClient(
        app,
        raise_server_exceptions=False,
    )

    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/operational-events"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert isinstance(
        body,
        list,
    )


def test_operations_center_routes_are_read_only() -> None:
    """
    Operations Center endpoints must remain GET-only.
    """

    app = create_app()

    client = TestClient(
        app,
        raise_server_exceptions=False,
    )

    dashboard_response = client.post(
        "/api/operations-center/dashboard"
    )

    events_response = client.post(
        "/api/operations-center/operational-events"
    )

    assert dashboard_response.status_code == 405
    assert events_response.status_code == 405

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\application\analytics\ai_platform\test_provider_analytics_dashboard_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
AI Platform Provider Analytics Dashboard Service tests.

Validates:

Provider Usage Events
        |
        +----------------+
        |                |
        v                v

Usage Analytics   Performance Analytics

        |
        v

Provider Analytics Dashboard Service

        |
        v

Unified Dashboard Contract


The dashboard analytics service does not:

- call providers;
- execute agents;
- persist data;
- render UI.
"""

from decimal import Decimal


from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.repository import (
    InMemoryProviderUsageRepository,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import (
    ProviderUsageEvent,
)

from app.application.analytics.ai_platform.provider_analytics_service import (
    ProviderAnalyticsService,
)

from app.application.analytics.ai_platform.provider_performance_service import (
    ProviderPerformanceService,
)

from app.application.analytics.ai_platform.provider_analytics_dashboard_service import (
    ProviderAnalyticsDashboardService,
)


def create_event(
    *,
    provider: str,
    latency_ms: int,
    success: bool = True,
) -> ProviderUsageEvent:
    """
    Create deterministic provider usage event.
    """

    return ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider=provider,
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        functionality="chat",
        agent="conversation_agent",
        input_tokens=1000,
        output_tokens=500,
        latency_ms=latency_ms,
        estimated_cost=Decimal(
            "0.010"
        ),
        quality_score=95.0,
        success=success,
    )


def create_dashboard_service(
    repository: InMemoryProviderUsageRepository,
) -> ProviderAnalyticsDashboardService:
    """
    Create dashboard analytics service.
    """

    return ProviderAnalyticsDashboardService(
        analytics_service=(
            ProviderAnalyticsService(
                repository=repository,
            )
        ),
        performance_service=(
            ProviderPerformanceService(
                repository=repository,
            )
        ),
    )


def test_provider_dashboard_combines_usage_cost_and_performance() -> None:
    """
    Validate unified dashboard aggregation.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
    )

    repository.save(
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            latency_ms=800,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            latency_ms=1200,
        )
    )

    service = create_dashboard_service(
        repository,
    )

    dashboard = (
        service.generate_dashboard()
    )

    assert dashboard.total_requests == 2

    assert dashboard.total_tokens == 3000

    assert dashboard.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0.020"
    )

    assert dashboard.providers == (
        "gemini",
        "openai",
    )

    assert dashboard.average_latency_ms == 1000.0

    assert dashboard.success_rate == 100.0


def test_provider_dashboard_handles_failures() -> None:
    """
    Validate failure aggregation.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
    )

    repository.save(
        create_event(
            provider="ollama",
            latency_ms=1500,
            success=False,
        )
    )

    service = create_dashboard_service(
        repository,
    )

    dashboard = (
        service.generate_dashboard()
    )

    assert dashboard.total_requests == 1

    assert dashboard.success_rate == 0.0

    assert dashboard.average_latency_ms == 1500.0


def test_empty_provider_dashboard() -> None:
    """
    Validate empty dashboard state.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
    )

    service = create_dashboard_service(
        repository,
    )

    dashboard = (
        service.generate_dashboard()
    )

    assert dashboard.total_requests == 0

    assert dashboard.total_tokens == 0

    assert dashboard.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0"
    )

    assert dashboard.providers == ()

    assert dashboard.average_latency_ms == 0.0

    assert dashboard.success_rate == 0.0

"""
AI Platform Provider Analytics Service tests.

Validates:

Provider Usage Events
        |
        v
Provider Usage Repository
        |
        v
Provider Analytics Service
        |
        v
Operational Analytics Summary


The analytics service does not:

- call providers;
- execute agents;
- access databases directly;
- modify runtime usage data.
"""

from decimal import Decimal


from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import (
    ProviderUsageEvent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.repository import (
    InMemoryProviderUsageRepository,
)

from app.application.analytics.ai_platform.provider_analytics_service import (
    ProviderAnalyticsService,
)


def create_repository() -> InMemoryProviderUsageRepository:
    """
    Create deterministic analytics repository.
    """

    return InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()


def create_event(
    *,
    provider: str,
    success: bool = True,
) -> ProviderUsageEvent:
    """
    Create deterministic provider usage event.
    """

    return ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider=provider,
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        functionality="chat",
        agent="conversation_agent",
        input_tokens=1000,
        output_tokens=500,
        latency_ms=800,
        estimated_cost=Decimal(
            "0.010"
        ),
        quality_score=95.0,
        success=success,
    )


def test_provider_analytics_generates_summary() -> None:
    """
    Validate analytics aggregation.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    repository.save(
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
        )
    )

    service = ProviderAnalyticsService(
        repository=repository,
    )

    summary = service.generate_summary()

    assert summary.total_requests == 2

    assert summary.total_tokens == 3000

    assert summary.providers == (
        "gemini",
        "openai",
    )

    assert summary.successful_requests == 2

    assert summary.failed_requests == 0

    assert summary.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0.020"
    )

    assert summary.success_rate == 100.0


def test_provider_analytics_counts_failed_requests() -> None:
    """
    Validate failure analytics.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    repository.save(
        create_event(
            provider="ollama",
            success=False,
        )
    )

    service = ProviderAnalyticsService(
        repository=repository,
    )

    summary = service.generate_summary()

    assert summary.total_requests == 1

    assert summary.failed_requests == 1

    assert summary.successful_requests == 0

    assert summary.success_rate == 0.0


def test_empty_repository_returns_empty_summary() -> None:
    """
    Validate empty analytics state.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    service = ProviderAnalyticsService(
        repository=repository,
    )

    summary = service.generate_summary()

    assert summary.total_requests == 0

    assert summary.total_tokens == 0

    assert summary.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0"
    )

    assert summary.providers == ()

"""

    *,
    latency_ms: int,

    return ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider="openai",

        capability="conversation",

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\application\provider_management\test_capability_optimisation_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center capability optimisation tests.

Validates:

Provider Usage
        |
        v
Provider Comparison Analytics
        |
        v
Capability Optimisation
        |
        v
Recommendation
        |
        v
Human Review

The optimisation service is advisory only.

It must not:

- change provider routing;
- execute providers;
- approve recommendations;
- send notifications.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.capability_optimisation_service import (
    CapabilityOptimisationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_comparison_service import (
    ProviderComparisonService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import (
    ProviderUsageEvent,
)


def create_event(
    *,
    provider: str,
    model: str,
    capability: str,
    latency_ms: int,
    cost: str,
    success: bool = True,
) -> ProviderUsageEvent:
    """
    Create deterministic provider usage evidence.
    """

    return ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider=provider,
        model=model,
        capability=capability,
        functionality="chat",
        agent="conversation_agent",
        input_tokens=1000,
        output_tokens=500,
        latency_ms=latency_ms,
        estimated_cost=Decimal(cost),
        quality_score=95.0,
        success=success,
    )


def test_manual_recommendation_contract_remains_supported() -> None:
    """
    Validate the original recommendation construction
    contract remains available.
    """

    service = CapabilityOptimisationService()

    recommendation = service.recommend(
        capability="conversation",
        current_provider="gemini",
        current_model="gemini-2.5-flash",
        recommended_provider="openai",
        recommended_model="gpt-5-mini",
        reason=(
            "Higher observed operational "
            "performance."
        ),
        confidence=96.0,
    )

    assert recommendation.capability == "conversation"

    assert (
        recommendation.current_provider
        == "gemini"
    )

    assert (
        recommendation.recommended_provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        recommendation.recommended_model
        == "gpt-5-mini"
    )

    assert recommendation.confidence == 96.0


def test_recommendation_is_generated_from_comparison_evidence() -> None:
    """
    Validate that provider comparison evidence can
    produce an optimisation recommendation.
    """

    comparison_service = (
        ProviderComparisonService()
    )

    optimisation_service = (
        CapabilityOptimisationService()
    )

    events = [
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            latency_ms=800,
            cost="0.010",
            success=True,
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            latency_ms=850,
            cost="0.010",
            success=True,
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="conversation",
            latency_ms=1400,
            cost="0.015",
            success=False,
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="conversation",
            latency_ms=1300,
            cost="0.015",
            success=True,
        ),
    ]

    comparisons = comparison_service.compare(
        events=events
    )

    recommendation = (
        optimisation_service
        .recommend_from_comparisons(
            capability="conversation",
            current_provider="gemini",
            current_model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            comparisons=comparisons,
        )
    )

    assert (
        recommendation.capability
        == "conversation"
    )

    assert (
        recommendation.current_provider
        == "gemini"
    )

    assert (
        recommendation.current_model
        == "gemini-2.5-flash"
    )

    assert (
        recommendation.recommended_provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        recommendation.recommended_model
        == "gpt-5-mini"
    )

    assert recommendation.confidence > 0.0

    assert recommendation.confidence <= 99.0

    assert (
        "strongest observed"
        in recommendation.reason
    )


def test_optimisation_is_capability_specific() -> None:
    """
    Validate that optimisation does not mix unrelated
    capabilities.

    Conversation evidence makes OpenAI stronger.

    Summarisation evidence makes Gemini stronger.

    The optimiser must evaluate each capability
    independently.
    """

    comparison_service = (
        ProviderComparisonService()
    )

    optimisation_service = (
        CapabilityOptimisationService()
    )

    events = [
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            latency_ms=800,
            cost="0.010",
            success=True,
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="conversation",
            latency_ms=1400,
            cost="0.015",
            success=True,
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="summarisation",
            latency_ms=600,
            cost="0.006",
            success=True,
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="ollama",
            model="gemma4",
            capability="summarisation",
            latency_ms=1500,
            cost="0.000",
            success=True,
        ),
    ]

    comparisons = comparison_service.compare(
        events=events
    )

    conversation_recommendation = (
        optimisation_service
        .recommend_from_comparisons(
            capability="conversation",
            current_provider="gemini",
            current_model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            comparisons=comparisons,
        )
    )

    summarisation_recommendation = (
        optimisation_service
        .recommend_from_comparisons(
            capability="summarisation",
            current_provider="ollama",
            current_model="gemma4",
            comparisons=comparisons,
        )
    )

    assert (
        conversation_recommendation.capability
        == "conversation"
    )

    assert (
        summarisation_recommendation.capability
        == "summarisation"
    )

    assert (
        conversation_recommendation
        .recommended_provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        summarisation_recommendation
        .recommended_provider
        == "gemini"
    )


def test_current_provider_can_remain_recommended() -> None:
    """
    Validate that optimisation does not force a change.

    If the current provider is already strongest,
    the recommendation should keep it.
    """

    comparison_service = (
        ProviderComparisonService()
    )

    optimisation_service = (
        CapabilityOptimisationService()
    )

    events = [
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            latency_ms=700,
            cost="0.010",
            success=True,
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="conversation",
            latency_ms=1500,
            cost="0.020",
            success=False,
        ),
    ]

    comparisons = comparison_service.compare(
        events=events
    )

    recommendation = (
        optimisation_service
        .recommend_from_comparisons(
            capability="conversation",
            current_provider="openai",
            current_model="gpt-5-mini",
            comparisons=comparisons,
        )
    )

    assert (
        recommendation.recommended_provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        recommendation.recommended_model
        == "gpt-5-mini"
    )

    assert (
        "Current provider remains"
        in recommendation.reason
    )


def test_no_comparison_data_is_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate missing optimisation evidence.
    """

    service = (
        CapabilityOptimisationService()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "No provider comparison data available"
        ),
    ):
        service.recommend_from_comparisons(
            capability="conversation",

def create_escalation() -> ProviderEscalation:

        InMemoryEmailNotificationAdapter()
    )

        InMemoryEmailNotificationAdapter()
    )

"""

        |

        |

AI Operations Center

    EscalationDetailService,
)

    InMemoryEscalationRepository,
)

    EscalationService,

    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationStatus,
    EscalationType,

def create_escalation_service() -> tuple[
    EscalationService,
    InMemoryEscalationRepository,
]:
    """
    Create an escalation service with an in-memory repository.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    service = EscalationService(
        repository=repository,
    )

    return service, repository


def test_get_detail_returns_escalation_information() -> None:
    """
    Validate retrieval of a complete escalation detail.
    """

    escalation_service, repository = (
        create_escalation_service()
    )

    escalation = (
        escalation_service.create_escalation(
            escalation_type=(
                EscalationType.LATENCY_DEGRADATION
            ),
            severity=(
                EscalationSeverity.HIGH
            ),
            provider="openai",
            capability="conversation",
            message=(
                "GPT-5-mini latency increased "
                "significantly."
            ),
            recommendation=(
                "Review alternative providers "
                "for conversation workloads."
            ),
        )
    )

    detail_service = (
        EscalationDetailService(
            repository=repository,
        )
    )

    detail = (
        detail_service.get_detail(
            escalation.escalation_id
        )
    )

    assert detail is not None

    assert (
        detail.escalation_id
        == escalation.escalation_id
    )

    assert (
        detail.escalation_type
        == EscalationType.LATENCY_DEGRADATION
    )

    assert (
        detail.severity
        == EscalationSeverity.HIGH
    )

    assert detail.provider == "openai"

    assert (
        detail.capability
        == "conversation"
    )

    assert (
        detail.message
        == (
            "GPT-5-mini latency increased "
            "significantly."
        )
    )

    assert (
        detail.recommendation
        == (
            "Review alternative providers "
            "for conversation workloads."
        )
    )

    assert (
        detail.status
        == EscalationStatus.OPEN
    )


def test_get_detail_preserves_created_at() -> None:
    """
    Validate preservation of the escalation timestamp.
    """

    escalation_service, repository = (
        create_escalation_service()
    )

    escalation = (
        escalation_service.create_escalation(
            escalation_type=(
                EscalationType.COST_SPIKE
            ),
            severity=(
                EscalationSeverity.MEDIUM
            ),
            provider="gemini",
            capability="summarisation",
            message=(
                "Provider cost increased."
            ),
        )
    )

    detail_service = (
        EscalationDetailService(
            repository=repository,
        )
    )

    detail = (
        detail_service.get_detail(
            escalation.escalation_id
        )
    )

    assert detail is not None

    assert (
        detail.created_at
        == escalation.created_at
    )

    assert (
        detail.created_at.tzinfo
        == timezone.utc
    )

def test_empty_escalation_id_is_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate escalation identifier input.
    """

    _, repository = (
        create_escalation_service()
    )

    detail_service = (
        EscalationDetailService(
            repository=repository,
        )
    )

        ValueError,
        match=(
