        ),
    ):
        detail_service.get_detail("")


def test_detail_service_is_read_only() -> None:
    """
    Validate that reading escalation details does not
    remove or modify the repository record.
    """

    escalation_service, repository = (
        create_escalation_service()
    )

    escalation = (
        escalation_service.create_escalation(
            escalation_type=(
                EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
            ),
            severity=(
                EscalationSeverity.CRITICAL
            ),
            provider="ollama",
            capability="conversation",
            message=(
                "Provider execution failed."
            ),
            recommendation=(
                "Review provider availability."
            ),
        )
    )

    detail_service = (
        EscalationDetailService(
            repository=repository,
        )
    )

    detail_service.get_detail(
        escalation.escalation_id
    )

    stored = repository.get_by_id(
        escalation.escalation_id
    )

    assert stored is not None

    assert (
        stored.escalation_id
        == escalation.escalation_id
    )

    assert (
        stored.status
        == EscalationStatus.OPEN
    )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center escalation history tests.

Validates the read-only application contract used by
the Operations Center.

Coverage:

- complete escalation history;
- individual workflow retrieval;
- status filtering;
- pending approval;
- approved history;
- rejected history;
- pending notification;
- status counts;
- pending approval detection.

The service must not mutate workflow state.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_history_service import (
    EscalationHistoryService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_workflow_repository import (
    InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_workflow_service import (
    EscalationWorkflow,
    EscalationWorkflowStatus,
)


def create_workflow(
    *,
    escalation_id: str,
    status: EscalationWorkflowStatus,
    minute: int,
) -> EscalationWorkflow:
    """
    Create deterministic workflow history data.
    """

    return EscalationWorkflow(
        escalation_id=escalation_id,
        status=status,
        created_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            minute,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        notification_recipient=(
            "operations@example.com"
            if status
            != EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
            else None
        ),
        notification_subject=(
            "EasyChatBot AI Operations Escalation"
            if status
            != EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
            else None
        ),
        approved_at=(
            datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                14,
                15,
                minute,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            )
            if status
            == EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .APPROVED
            else None
        ),
        rejected_at=(
            datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                14,
                15,
                minute,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            )
            if status
            == EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .REJECTED
            else None
        ),
        decision_reason=(
            "Operations decision recorded."
            if status
            in (
                EscalationWorkflowStatus.APPROVED,
                EscalationWorkflowStatus.REJECTED,
            )
            else None
        ),
    )


def create_service() -> (
    tuple[
        EscalationHistoryService,
        InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository,
    ]
):
    """
    Create a history service and repository.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository()
    )

    service = EscalationHistoryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    return service, repository


def populate_repository(
    repository: InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository,
) -> None:
    """
    Populate deterministic escalation history.
    """

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
            ),
            minute=30,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-002",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .AWAITING_APPROVAL
            ),
            minute=31,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-003",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .APPROVED
            ),
            minute=32,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-004",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .REJECTED
            ),
            minute=33,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-005",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .AWAITING_APPROVAL
            ),
            minute=34,
        )
    )


def test_list_history_returns_all_workflows() -> None:
    """
    Validate complete history retrieval.
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    populate_repository(repository)

    history = service.list_history()

    assert len(history) == 5

    assert [
        workflow.escalation_id
        for workflow in history
    ] == [
        "esc-001",
        "esc-002",
        "esc-003",
        "esc-004",
        "esc-005",
    ]


def test_get_returns_requested_workflow() -> None:
    """
    Validate individual workflow retrieval.
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    populate_repository(repository)

    workflow = service.get(
        "esc-003"
    )

    assert workflow is not None

    assert (
        workflow.escalation_id
        == "esc-003"
    )

    assert (
        workflow.status
        == EscalationWorkflowStatus
        .APPROVED
    )


def test_get_returns_none_for_unknown_workflow() -> None:
    """
    Validate unknown workflow behaviour.
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    populate_repository(repository)

    assert (
        service.get("esc-404")
        is None
    )


def test_get_rejects_empty_escalation_id() -> None:
    """
    Validate workflow identity.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Escalation ID cannot be empty.",
    ):
        service.get("   ")


def test_list_by_status_filters_correctly() -> None:

    Validate generic status filtering.
    """

        .AWAITING_APPROVAL

        workflow.escalation_id

        )

    populate_repository(repository)

    counts = service.count_by_status()

    assert (
        counts[
            EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
        ]

            .APPROVED
        ]

            EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .REJECTED
        ]
        == 1
    )


def test_count_by_status_includes_zero_counts() -> None:
    """
    Validate that all lifecycle statuses are represented
    even when no workflows exist for a status.
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .APPROVED
            ),
            minute=30,
        )
    )

    counts = service.count_by_status()

    assert (
        counts[
            EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .APPROVED
        ]
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        counts[
            EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
        ]
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        counts[
            EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .AWAITING_APPROVAL
        ]
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        counts[
            EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .REJECTED
        ]
        == 0
    )

    )

    repository.save(

            escalation_id="esc-001",

                .APPROVED
            ),

    )

        service.has_pending_approval()
        is False
    )


def test_history_service_is_read_only() -> None:
    """
    Validate that querying history does not change
    repository state.
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    populate_repository(repository)

    before = repository.get_all()

    service.list_history()
    service.list_pending_approval()
    service.list_approved()
    service.list_rejected()
    service.list_pending_notification()
    service.count_by_status()
    service.has_pending_approval()

    after = repository.get_all()

    assert before == after

Validates:

Provider Escalation
        |
        v
EscalationNotificationService
        |

Email Notification Adapter
        |

Prepared Human Notification

No provider routing is changed.

No escalation is approved automatically.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.email_notification_adapter import (
    InMemoryEmailNotificationAdapter,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_notification_service import (
    EscalationNotificationService,

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationType,
    ProviderEscalation,
)


def create_escalation() -> ProviderEscalation:
    """
    Create a deterministic provider failure escalation.
    """

    return ProviderEscalation(
        escalation_id="esc-001",
        escalation_type=(
            EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.HIGH
        ),
        provider="openai",
        capability="conversation",
        message=(
            "Provider openai is currently unavailable. "
            "Affected capabilities: conversation. "
            "Consecutive health failures: 3."
        ),
        recommendation=(
            "Review alternative providers for "
            "conversation workloads. "
            "Human approval is required."
        ),
        created_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            34,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )


def create_service() -> (
    EscalationNotificationService
):
    """
    Create the notification service with the in-memory
    email adapter.
    """

    adapter = (
        InMemoryEmailNotificationAdapter()
    )

    return EscalationNotificationService(
        email_adapter=adapter
    )


def test_escalation_notification_is_prepared() -> None:
    """
    Validate preparation of a human escalation
    notification.
    """

    service = create_service()

    result = (
        service.prepare_escalation_notification(
            escalation=create_escalation(),
            recipient="operations@example.com",
        )
    )

    assert (
        result.escalation_id
        == "esc-001"
    )

    assert (
        result.recipient
        == "operations@example.com"
    )

    assert (
        "AI Operations Escalation"
        in result.subject
    )

    assert (
        "HIGH"
        in result.subject
    )

    assert (
        "esc-001"
        in result.body
    )

    assert (
        "provider_failure"
        in result.body
    )

    assert (
        "openai"
        in result.body
    )

    assert (
        "conversation"
        in result.body
    )

    the immutable escalation contract.

    service = create_service()

        == escalation.escalation_id

        escalation.provider
        == "openai"

        escalation.severity
        == EscalationSeverity.HIGH

        == EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE

def test_create_escalation_persists_record() -> None:
    """
    Validate escalation creation and persistence.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    service = EscalationService(
        repository=repository,
    )

    escalation = service.create_escalation(
        escalation_type=(
            EscalationType.LATENCY_DEGRADATION
        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.HIGH
        ),
        provider="openai",
        capability="conversation",
        message=(
            "GPT-5-mini latency increased "
            "significantly."
        ),
        recommendation=(
            "Review alternative providers "
            "for conversation workloads."
        ),
    )

    assert escalation.escalation_id

    assert (
        escalation.escalation_type
        == EscalationType.LATENCY_DEGRADATION
    )

    assert (
        escalation.severity
        == EscalationSeverity.HIGH
    )

    assert escalation.provider == "openai"

    assert (
        escalation.capability
        == "conversation"
    )

    assert escalation.recommendation == (
        "Review alternative providers "
        "for conversation workloads."
    )

    assert (
        escalation.status
        == EscalationStatus.OPEN
    )

    stored = repository.get_by_id(
        escalation.escalation_id
    )

    assert stored == escalation

        InMemoryEscalationRepository()

        repository=repository,

        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.CRITICAL
        ),
        provider="ollama",
        capability="conversation",
        message=(
            "Provider execution failed."
        ),
    )

    history = service.list_escalations()

    assert len(history) == 2

    assert history[0] == first

    assert history[1] == second

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center escalation type tests.

Validates:

Escalation Repository
        |
        v
EscalationTypeService
        |
        v
EscalationTypeSummary
        |
        v
AI Operations Center

The service is read-only.

It does not:

- create escalations;
- modify escalations;
- approve recommendations;
- send emails;
- change provider routing.
"""

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_repository import (
    InMemoryEscalationRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_service import (
    EscalationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_type_service import (
    EscalationTypeService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationStatus,
    EscalationType,
)


def create_services() -> tuple[
    EscalationService,
    EscalationTypeService,
    InMemoryEscalationRepository,
]:
    """
    Create the escalation services and repository.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    escalation_service = EscalationService(
        repository=repository,
    )

    type_service = EscalationTypeService(
        repository=repository,
    )

    return (
        escalation_service,
        type_service,
        repository,
    )


def test_summary_contains_all_escalation_types() -> None:
    """
    Validate that every supported escalation type
    is represented, including types with zero incidents.
    """

    (
        _,
        type_service,
        _,
    ) = create_services()

    summaries = (
        type_service.get_summary()
    )

    types = tuple(
        summary.escalation_type
        for summary in summaries
    )

    assert types == tuple(
        EscalationType
    )

    assert len(summaries) == len(
        EscalationType
    )


def test_summary_counts_escalations_by_type() -> None:
    """
    Validate aggregation of escalation counts.
    """

    (
        escalation_service,
        type_service,
        _,
    ) = create_services()

    escalation_service.create_escalation(
        escalation_type=(
            EscalationType.COST_SPIKE
        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.MEDIUM
        ),
        provider="openai",
        capability="conversation",
        message="Cost increased.",
    )

    escalation_service.create_escalation(
        escalation_type=(
            EscalationType.COST_SPIKE
        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.HIGH
        ),
        provider="openai",
        capability="research",
        message="Research cost increased.",
    )

    escalation_service.create_escalation(
        escalation_type=(
            EscalationType.QUALITY_DROP
        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.HIGH
        ),
        provider="gemini",
        capability="summarisation",
        message="Quality decreased.",
    )

    summaries = (
        type_service.get_summary()
    )

    summary_by_type = {
        summary.escalation_type: summary
        for summary in summaries
    }

    assert (
        summary_by_type[
            EscalationType.COST_SPIKE
        ].total_count
        == 2
    )

    assert (
        summary_by_type[
            EscalationType.QUALITY_DROP
        ].total_count
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        summary_by_type[
            EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
        ].total_count
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        summary_by_type[
            EscalationType.LATENCY_DEGRADATION
        ].total_count
        == 0
    )


def test_summary_counts_only_open_and_acknowledged_as_active() -> None:
    """
    Validate that resolved and dismissed escalations
    are excluded from active counts.
    """

    (
        escalation_service,
        type_service,
        repository,
    ) = create_services()

    open_escalation = (
        escalation_service.create_escalation(
            escalation_type=(
                EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
            ),
            severity=(
                EscalationSeverity.CRITICAL
            ),
            provider="ollama",
            capability="conversation",
            message="Provider unavailable.",
        )
    )

    acknowledged_escalation = (
        escalation_service.create_escalation(
            escalation_type=(
                EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
            ),
            severity=(
                EscalationSeverity.HIGH
            ),
            provider="ollama",
            capability="research",
            message="Provider degraded.",
        )
    )

    resolved_escalation = (
        escalation_service.create_escalation(
            escalation_type=(
                EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
            ),
            severity=(
                EscalationSeverity.HIGH
            ),
            provider="ollama",
            capability="conversation",
            message="Provider was unavailable.",
        )
    )

    dismissed_escalation = (
        escalation_service.create_escalation(
            escalation_type=(
                EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
            ),
            severity=(
                EscalationSeverity.MEDIUM
            ),
            provider="ollama",
            capability="research",
            message="Transient provider issue.",
        )
    )

    repository.clear()

    repository.save(
        open_escalation
    )

    repository.save(
        acknowledged_escalation.__class__(
            escalation_id=(
                acknowledged_escalation.escalation_id
            ),
            escalation_type=(
                acknowledged_escalation.escalation_type
            ),
            severity=(
                acknowledged_escalation.severity
            ),
            provider=(
                acknowledged_escalation.provider
            ),
            capability=(
                acknowledged_escalation.capability
            ),
            message=(
                acknowledged_escalation.message
            ),
            recommendation=(
                acknowledged_escalation.recommendation
            ),
            created_at=(
                acknowledged_escalation.created_at
            ),
            status=(
                EscalationStatus.ACKNOWLEDGED
            ),
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        resolved_escalation.__class__(
            escalation_id=(
                resolved_escalation.escalation_id
            ),
            escalation_type=(
                resolved_escalation.escalation_type
            ),
            severity=(
                resolved_escalation.severity
            ),
            provider=(
                resolved_escalation.provider
            ),
            capability=(
                resolved_escalation.capability
            ),
            message=(
                resolved_escalation.message
            ),
            recommendation=(
                resolved_escalation.recommendation
            ),
            created_at=(
                resolved_escalation.created_at
            ),
            status=(
                EscalationStatus.RESOLVED
            ),
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        dismissed_escalation.__class__(
            escalation_id=(
                dismissed_escalation.escalation_id
            ),
            escalation_type=(
                dismissed_escalation.escalation_type
            ),
            severity=(
                dismissed_escalation.severity
            ),
            provider=(
                dismissed_escalation.provider
            ),
            capability=(
                dismissed_escalation.capability
            ),
            message=(
                dismissed_escalation.message
            ),
            recommendation=(
                dismissed_escalation.recommendation
            ),
            created_at=(
                dismissed_escalation.created_at
            ),
            status=(
                EscalationStatus.DISMISSED
            ),
        )
    )

    summaries = (
        type_service.get_summary()
    )

    summary_by_type = {
        summary.escalation_type: summary
        for summary in summaries
    }

    provider_failure = summary_by_type[
        EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
    ]

    assert provider_failure.total_count == 4

    assert provider_failure.active_count == 2


def test_empty_repository_returns_zero_counts() -> None:
    """
    Validate the empty Operations Center state.
    """

    (
        _,
        type_service,
        _,
    ) = create_services()

    summaries = (
        type_service.get_summary()
    )

    for summary in summaries:
        assert summary.total_count == 0
        assert summary.active_count == 0

    """
    Validate that generating the summary does not
    modify repository data.

    (
        escalation_service,
        type_service,
        repository,
    ) = create_services()

            ),
            provider="openai",
            capability="conversation",

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center escalation workflow
repository tests.

Validates:

EscalationWorkflowService
        |
        v
EscalationWorkflowRepository
        |
        v
In-Memory Persistence
        |
        v
Escalation History

The repository does not:

- make business decisions;
- send notifications;
- approve escalations;
- modify Runtime routing;
- execute provider changes.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_workflow_repository import (
    InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_workflow_service import (
    EscalationWorkflow,
    EscalationWorkflowStatus,
)


def create_workflow(
    *,
    escalation_id: str = "esc-001",
    status: EscalationWorkflowStatus = (
        EscalationWorkflowStatus.PENDING_NOTIFICATION
    ),
    created_at: datetime | None = None,
) -> EscalationWorkflow:
    """
    Create a deterministic workflow record.
    """

    if created_at is None:
        created_at = datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            30,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )

    return EscalationWorkflow(
        escalation_id=escalation_id,
        status=status,
        created_at=created_at,
        notification_recipient=None,
        notification_subject=None,
        approved_at=None,
        rejected_at=None,
        decision_reason=None,
    )


def create_repository() -> (
    InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository
):
    """
    Create a fresh repository.
    """

    return InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository()


def test_workflow_can_be_saved_and_retrieved() -> None:
    """
    Validate basic persistence and retrieval.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    workflow = create_workflow()

    repository.save(workflow)

    retrieved = repository.get(
        "esc-001"
    )

    assert retrieved is not None

    assert (
        retrieved.escalation_id
        == "esc-001"
    )

    assert (
        retrieved.status
        == EscalationWorkflowStatus
        .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
    )

    assert (
        retrieved.created_at
        == workflow.created_at
    )


def test_saving_existing_workflow_replaces_previous_state() -> None:
    """
    Validate workflow state updates.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    created_at = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        30,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    pending = create_workflow(
        escalation_id="esc-001",
        status=(
            EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
        ),
        created_at=created_at,
    )

    repository.save(pending)

    approved = EscalationWorkflow(
        escalation_id="esc-001",
        status=(
            EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .APPROVED
        ),
        created_at=created_at,
        notification_recipient=(
            "operations@example.com"
        ),
        notification_subject=(
            "EasyChatBot AI Operations Escalation"
        ),
        approved_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            40,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        rejected_at=None,
        decision_reason=(
            "Approved by Operations."
        ),
    )

    repository.save(approved)

    retrieved = repository.get(
        "esc-001"
    )

    assert retrieved is not None

    assert (
        retrieved.status
        == EscalationWorkflowStatus
        .APPROVED
    )

    assert (
        retrieved.notification_recipient
        == "operations@example.com"
    )

    assert (
        retrieved.decision_reason
        == "Approved by Operations."
    )

    assert (
        retrieved.approved_at
        is not None
    )

    repository = create_repository()

    result = repository.get(
        "esc-404"
    )

    assert result is None

def test_empty_escalation_id_is_rejected_on_save() -> None:
    """
    Validate workflow identity during persistence.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    workflow = create_workflow(
        escalation_id="   "

def test_multiple_workflows_can_be_persisted() -> None:
    """
    Validate multiple independent workflow records.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001"
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-002"
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-003"
        )
    )

    workflows = repository.get_all()

    assert len(workflows) == 3

    assert {
        workflow.escalation_id
        for workflow in workflows
    } == {
        "esc-001",
        "esc-002",
        "esc-003",
    }


def test_workflows_are_returned_in_creation_order() -> None:
    """
    Validate deterministic history ordering.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    first_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        30,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    second_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        31,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    third_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        32,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-003",
            created_at=third_time,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            created_at=first_time,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-002",
            created_at=second_time,
        )
    )

    workflows = repository.get_all()

    assert [
        workflow.escalation_id
        for workflow in workflows
    ] == [
        "esc-001",
        "esc-002",
        "esc-003",
    ]


def test_get_all_returns_empty_collection_initially() -> None:
    """
    Validate empty repository behaviour.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    assert repository.get_all() == []


def test_notification_state_is_persisted() -> None:
    """
    Validate persistence of notification metadata.
    """

    repository = create_repository()

    created_at = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        30,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    notification_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        35,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    workflow = EscalationWorkflow(
        escalation_id="esc-001",
        status=(
            EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .AWAITING_APPROVAL
        ),
        created_at=created_at,
        notification_recipient=(
            "operations@example.com"
        ),
        notification_subject=(
            "EasyChatBot AI Operations Escalation"
        ),
        approved_at=None,
        rejected_at=None,
        decision_reason=None,
    )

    repository.save(workflow)

    retrieved = repository.get(
        "esc-001"
    )

        retrieved.status
        == EscalationWorkflowStatus
        .AWAITING_APPROVAL
    )

    assert (
        retrieved.notification_recipient
        == "operations@example.com"

        == "EasyChatBot AI Operations Escalation"

        retrieved.approved_at
        is None
    )

    assert (
        retrieved.rejected_at
        is None
    )

    # Keep the timestamp variable explicitly used so this
    # test documents the intended notification timeline.
    assert notification_time > created_at

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center escalation workflow tests.

Validates the human-in-the-loop lifecycle:

Escalation
    |
    v
PENDING_NOTIFICATION
    |
    v
AWAITING_APPROVAL
    |
    +------------------+
    |                  |
    v                  v
 APPROVED           REJECTED

Approval records a human decision only.

It does NOT execute provider changes,
modify Runtime routing, or perform failover.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.email_notification_adapter import (
    InMemoryEmailNotificationAdapter,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_notification_service import (
    EscalationNotificationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_workflow_service import (
    EscalationWorkflowService,
    EscalationWorkflowStatus,
)

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationType,
    ProviderEscalation,
)


def create_escalation() -> ProviderEscalation:
    """
    Create a deterministic escalation.
    """

    return ProviderEscalation(
        escalation_id="esc-001",
        escalation_type=(
            EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.HIGH
        ),
        provider="openai",
        capability="conversation",
        message=(
            "Provider openai is currently unavailable."
        ),
        recommendation=(
            "Review alternative providers for "
            "conversation workloads."
        ),
        created_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            34,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )


def create_service() -> EscalationWorkflowService:
    """
    Create a workflow service using the in-memory
    notification adapter.
    """

    adapter = (
        InMemoryEmailNotificationAdapter()
    )

    notification_service = (
        EscalationNotificationService(
            email_adapter=adapter
        )
    )

    return EscalationWorkflowService(
        notification_service=(
            notification_service
        )
    )


def test_workflow_starts_pending_notification() -> None:
    """
    Validate initial workflow state.
    """

    service = create_service()

    workflow = service.create_workflow(
        escalation=create_escalation()
    )

    assert (
        workflow.escalation_id
        == "esc-001"
    )

    assert (
        workflow.status
        == EscalationWorkflowStatus
        .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
    )

    assert (
        workflow.notification_recipient
        is None
    )

    assert (
        workflow.approved_at
        is None
    )

    assert (
        workflow.rejected_at
        is None
    )


def test_notification_moves_workflow_to_awaiting_approval() -> None:
    """
    Validate notification preparation and workflow
    transition.
    """

    service = create_service()

    escalation = create_escalation()

    service.create_workflow(
        escalation=escalation
    )

    notification = (
        service.prepare_notification(
            escalation=escalation,
            recipient="operations@example.com",
        )
    )

    assert (
        notification.recipient
        == "operations@example.com"
    )

    workflow = service.get_workflow(
        "esc-001"
    )

    assert workflow is not None

    assert (
        workflow.status
        == EscalationWorkflowStatus
        .AWAITING_APPROVAL
    )

    assert (
        workflow.notification_recipient
        == "operations@example.com"
    )

    assert (
        workflow.notification_subject
        is not None
    )


def test_notification_can_create_workflow_automatically() -> None:

        is None

        == (
            "Operations determined that the "
            "provider issue is transient."
        )
    )


def test_approval_requires_awaiting_approval_state() -> None:
    """
    Validate that approval cannot happen before
    notification workflow state.
    """

    service = create_service()

    service.create_workflow(
        escalation=create_escalation()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Escalation must be awaiting approval "
            "before it can be approved."
        ),
    ):
        service.approve(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            reason="Approved.",
        )

    """
    Validate that rejection cannot happen before
    notification workflow state.

    service = create_service()

    service.create_workflow(
        escalation=create_escalation()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,

            "Escalation must be awaiting approval "

    Validate workflow retrieval.

    first = create_escalation()

    second = ProviderEscalation(
        escalation_id="esc-002",
        escalation_type=(
            EscalationType.LATENCY_DEGRADATION
        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.MEDIUM
        ),
        provider="gemini",
        capability="summarisation",
        message="Latency degraded.",
        recommendation="Review provider performance.",
        created_at=datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        ),
    )

    service.create_workflow(
        escalation=first
    )

    service.create_workflow(
        escalation=second
    )

    workflows = service.list_workflows()

    assert len(workflows) == 2

    assert {
        workflow.escalation_id
        for workflow in workflows
    } == {
        "esc-001",
        "esc-002",
    }

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center health dashboard tests.

Validates:

Provider Analytics
        |
        +----------------------+
        |                      |
        v                      v

Provider Health         Escalation Repository
        |                      |
        +----------+-----------+
                   |
                   v
        AIHealthDashboardService
                   |
                   v
        Dashboard Health Contract

No UI is rendered and no external systems are called.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import (
    ProviderUsageEvent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.repository import (
    InMemoryProviderUsageRepository,
)

from app.application.analytics.ai_platform.provider_analytics_service import (
    ProviderAnalyticsService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_repository import (
    InMemoryEscalationRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_service import (
    EscalationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.health_dashboard_service import (
    AIHealthDashboardService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationStatus,
    EscalationType,
)


def create_usage_event(
    *,
    provider: str,
    success: bool = True,
) -> ProviderUsageEvent:
    """
    Create deterministic provider usage data.
    """

    return ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider=provider,
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        functionality="chat",
        agent="conversation_agent",
        input_tokens=1000,
        output_tokens=500,
        latency_ms=800,
        estimated_cost=Decimal("0.010"),
        quality_score=95.0,
        success=success,
    )


def create_dashboard_service(
    usage_repository: InMemoryProviderUsageRepository,
    escalation_repository: InMemoryEscalationRepository,
) -> AIHealthDashboardService:
    """
    Create the dashboard service from its dependencies.
    """

    analytics_service = ProviderAnalyticsService(
        repository=usage_repository,
    )

    return AIHealthDashboardService(
        analytics_service=analytics_service,
        escalation_repository=escalation_repository,
    )


def test_health_dashboard_reports_provider_health() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider health information.
    """

    usage_repository = (
        InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
    )

    escalation_repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    usage_repository.save(
        create_usage_event(
            provider="openai",
        )
    )

    usage_repository.save(
        create_usage_event(
            provider="gemini",
        )
    )

    service = create_dashboard_service(
        usage_repository,
        escalation_repository,
    )

    dashboard = (
        service.generate_dashboard()
    )

    assert dashboard.system_confidence == 100.0

    assert dashboard.active_providers == (
        "gemini",
        "openai",
    )

    assert dashboard.monitoring_active is True

    assert dashboard.active_escalations == 0

    assert dashboard.high_priority_escalations == 0


def test_health_dashboard_counts_active_escalations() -> None:
    """
    Validate active escalation aggregation.
    """

    usage_repository = (
        InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
    )

    escalation_repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    usage_repository.save(
        create_usage_event(
            provider="openai",
        )
    )

    escalation_service = EscalationService(
        repository=escalation_repository,
    )

    escalation_service.create_escalation(
        escalation_type=(
            EscalationType.COST_SPIKE
        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.MEDIUM
        ),
        provider="openai",
        capability="conversation",
        message="Provider cost increased.",
    )

    escalation_service.create_escalation(
        escalation_type=(
            EscalationType.LATENCY_DEGRADATION
        ),
        severity=(
            EscalationSeverity.HIGH
        ),
        provider="openai",
        capability="conversation",
        message="Provider latency increased.",
    )

    service = create_dashboard_service(
        usage_repository,
        escalation_repository,
    )

    dashboard = (
        service.generate_dashboard()
    )

    assert dashboard.active_escalations == 2

    assert (
        dashboard.high_priority_escalations
        == 1
    )


def test_resolved_escalations_are_not_active() -> None:
    """
    Validate escalation lifecycle filtering.

    Resolved and dismissed escalations must not
    appear in the active dashboard count.
    """

    usage_repository = (
        InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
    )

    escalation_repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    usage_repository.save(
        create_usage_event(
            provider="openai",
        )
    )

    escalation_service = EscalationService(
        repository=escalation_repository,
    )

    escalation = (
        escalation_service.create_escalation(
            escalation_type=(
                EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
            ),
            severity=(
                EscalationSeverity.CRITICAL
            ),
            provider="openai",
            capability="conversation",
            message="Provider unavailable.",
        )
    )

    resolved_escalation = (
        escalation.__class__(
            escalation_id=(
                escalation.escalation_id
            ),
            escalation_type=(
                escalation.escalation_type
            ),
            severity=(
                escalation.severity
            ),
            provider=(
                escalation.provider
            ),
            capability=(
                escalation.capability
            ),
            message=(
                escalation.message
            ),
            recommendation=(
                escalation.recommendation
            ),
            created_at=(
                escalation.created_at
            ),
            status=(
                EscalationStatus.RESOLVED
            ),
        )
    )

    escalation_repository.clear()

    escalation_repository.save(
        resolved_escalation
    )

    service = create_dashboard_service(
        usage_repository,
        escalation_repository,
    )

    dashboard = (
        service.generate_dashboard()
    )

    assert dashboard.active_escalations == 0

    assert (
        dashboard.high_priority_escalations
        == 0
    )


def test_failed_provider_requests_reduce_system_confidence() -> None:
    """
    Validate that provider failures affect the current
    operational confidence metric.
    """

    usage_repository = (
        InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
    )

    escalation_repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    usage_repository.save(
        create_usage_event(
            provider="openai",
            success=True,
        )
    )

    usage_repository.save(
        create_usage_event(
            provider="openai",
            success=False,
        )
    )

    service = create_dashboard_service(
        usage_repository,
        escalation_repository,
    )

    dashboard = (
        service.generate_dashboard()
    )

    assert dashboard.system_confidence == 50.0

    assert dashboard.active_providers == (
        "openai",
    )

    assert dashboard.monitoring_active is True

"""
Tests for the OperationalEvent contract.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventSeverity,
    OperationalEventType,
)


def create_event() -> OperationalEvent:
    """
    Create deterministic operational event data.
    """

    return OperationalEvent.create(
        event_type=(
            OperationalEventType
            .AGENT_EXECUTION_FAILED
        ),
        severity=(
            OperationalEventSeverity.ERROR
        ),
        source="external_agent_runtime",
        component="conversation_agent",
        occurred_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            16,
            12,
            0,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        message="Agent execution failed.",
        correlation_id="run-001",
        metadata={

        metadata=metadata,

"""
Tests for the operational event repository.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventSeverity,
    OperationalEventType,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_repository import (
    InMemoryOperationalEventRepository,
)


def create_event(
    *,
    minute: int,
    message: str,
) -> OperationalEvent:
    """
    Create deterministic operational event data.
    """

    return OperationalEvent.create(
        event_type=(
            OperationalEventType
            .AGENT_EXECUTION_FAILED
        ),
        severity=(
            OperationalEventSeverity.ERROR
        ),
        source="external_agent_runtime",
        component="conversation_agent",
        occurred_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            16,
            12,
            minute,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        message=message,
        correlation_id="run-001",
        metadata={
            "provider": "openai",
        },
    )


def test_repository_starts_empty() -> None:
    """
    Validate initial repository state.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()
    )

    assert repository.get_all() == ()


def test_repository_saves_and_retrieves_event() -> None:
    """
    Validate persistence and retrieval by event ID.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()
    )

    event = create_event(
        minute=1,
        message="Execution failed.",
    )

    repository.save(event)

    assert (
        repository.get_by_id(
            event.event_id
        )
        == event
    )


def test_repository_returns_none_for_unknown_event() -> None:
    """
    Validate unknown event lookup.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()
    )

    event = create_event(
        minute=1,
        message="Execution failed.",
    )

    assert (
        repository.get_by_id(
            event.event_id
        )
        is None
    )


def test_repository_orders_events_deterministically() -> None:
    """
    Validate chronological event retrieval.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()
    )

    second = create_event(
        minute=2,
        message="Second event.",
    )

    first = create_event(
        minute=1,
        message="First event.",
    )

    repository.save(second)
    repository.save(first)

    events = repository.get_all()

    assert events == (
        first,
        second,
    )


def test_repository_replaces_event_with_same_id() -> None:
    """
    Validate repository identity semantics.

    Operational events normally have unique IDs. If the same
    ID is persisted again, the repository retains the latest
    supplied representation.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()
    )

    event = create_event(
        minute=1,
        message="Original event.",
    )

    repository.save(event)
    repository.save(event)

    events = repository.get_all()

    assert len(events) == 1
    assert events[0] == event


def test_none_event_is_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate repository input protection.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Operational event cannot be None.",
    ):
        repository.save(
            None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )


def test_none_event_id_is_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate event ID input protection.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Operational event ID cannot be None."
        ),
    ):
        repository.get_by_id(None)


def test_clear_removes_all_events() -> None:
    """
    Validate repository reset behaviour.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()
    )

    repository.save(
        create_event(
            minute=1,
            message="Execution failed.",
        )
    )

    repository.clear()

    assert repository.get_all() == ()

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center operational event service tests.

Validates the generic application boundary:

OperationalEvent
        |
        v
OperationalEventService
        |
        v
OperationalEventRepository

The service must:

- reject invalid dependencies;
- reject invalid events;
- persist immutable operational facts through
  the repository abstraction;
- retrieve events by ID;
- return events in repository order;
- expose an isolated clear operation.

The service must NOT:

- know about providers;
- know about incidents;
- execute Runtime operations;
- execute agents;
- perform autonomous actions;
- access persistence infrastructure directly.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventSeverity,
    OperationalEventType,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_repository import (
    InMemoryOperationalEventRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)


def create_event(
    *,
    event_type: OperationalEventType = (
        OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_HEALTH_CHANGED
    ),
    severity: OperationalEventSeverity = (
        OperationalEventSeverity.INFO
    ),
    source: str = "test",
    component: str = "test_component",
    minute: int = 0,
    correlation_id: str | None = "correlation-001",
    metadata: dict[str, object] | None = None,
) -> OperationalEvent:
    """
    Create a deterministic operational event.
    """

    return OperationalEvent.create(
        event_type=event_type,
        severity=severity,
        source=source,
        component=component,
        occurred_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            minute,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        message="Operational event test.",
        correlation_id=correlation_id,
        metadata=(
            metadata
            if metadata is not None
            else {
                "test": True,
            }
        ),
    )


def create_service() -> (
    tuple[
        OperationalEventService,
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository,
    ]
):
    """
    Create a service with an isolated in-memory repository.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()
    )

    service = OperationalEventService(
        repository=repository,
    )

    return service, repository


def test_service_requires_repository() -> None:
    """
    Validate the composition dependency boundary.
    """

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Operational event repository "
            "cannot be None."
        ),
    ):
        OperationalEventService(
            repository=None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )


def test_record_rejects_none_event() -> None:
    """
    Validate required event input.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Operational event cannot be None.",
    ):
        service.record(
            None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )


def test_record_persists_and_returns_same_event() -> None:
    """
    Validate immutable event persistence.

    The service must return the exact event supplied by
    the caller after successful persistence.
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    event = create_event()

    result = service.record(event)

    assert result is event

    stored = repository.get_by_id(
        event.event_id
    )

    assert stored is event


def test_get_event_returns_persisted_event() -> None:
    """
    Validate retrieval by event ID.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    event = create_event()

    service.record(event)

    result = service.get_event(
        event.event_id
    )

    assert result is event


def test_get_event_returns_none_for_unknown_id() -> None:
    """
    Validate unknown event lookup.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    unknown_id = UUID(
        "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001"
    )

    assert (
        service.get_event(
            unknown_id
        )
        is None
    )


def test_get_event_rejects_none_id() -> None:
    """
    Validate required event ID.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Operational event ID cannot be None."
        ),
    ):
        service.get_event(
            None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )


def test_list_events_returns_repository_order() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the application service does not
    reorder repository results.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    first = create_event(
        minute=1,
    )

    second = create_event(
        event_type=(
            OperationalEventType
            .PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED
        ),
        severity=(
            OperationalEventSeverity.CRITICAL
        ),
        minute=2,
    )

    third = create_event(
        event_type=(
            OperationalEventType
            .PROVIDER_RECOVERED
        ),
        minute=3,
    )

    service.record(first)
    service.record(second)
    service.record(third)

    events = service.list_events()

    assert events == (
        first,
        second,
        third,
    )


def test_list_events_is_empty_initially() -> None:
    """
    Validate isolated service state.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    assert service.list_events() == ()


def test_clear_removes_all_events() -> None:
    """
    Validate isolated state reset.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    first = create_event(
        minute=1,
    )

    second = create_event(
        minute=2,
    )

    service.record(first)
    service.record(second)

    assert len(
        service.list_events()
    ) == 2

    service.clear()

    assert service.list_events() == ()


def test_event_identity_and_metadata_are_preserved() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the generic service does not transform
    operational event content.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    event = create_event(
        source="provider_management",
        component="ProviderIncidentService",
        correlation_id="incident-001",
        metadata={
            "provider": "openai",
            "incident_id": "incident-001",
        },
    )

    result = service.record(event)

    assert result.event_id == event.event_id

    assert (
        result.event_type
        == OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_HEALTH_CHANGED
    )

    assert (
        result.severity
        == OperationalEventSeverity.INFO
    )

    assert (
        result.source
        == "provider_management"
    )

    assert (
        result.component
        == "ProviderIncidentService"
    )

    assert (
        result.correlation_id
        == "incident-001"
    )

    assert (
        result.metadata["provider"]
        == "openai"
    )


def test_service_remains_provider_agnostic() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the generic service can record different
    operational event categories without provider-specific
    branching.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    events = (
        create_event(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType
                .PROVIDER_HEALTH_CHANGED
            ),
        ),
        create_event(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType
                .PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED
            ),
            severity=(
                OperationalEventSeverity.CRITICAL
            ),
            minute=1,
        ),
        create_event(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType
                .PROVIDER_RECOVERED
            ),
            minute=2,
        ),
        create_event(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType
                .AGENT_EXECUTION_FAILED
            ),
            severity=(
                OperationalEventSeverity.ERROR
            ),
            minute=3,
        ),
    )

    for event in events:
        service.record(event)

    result = service.list_events()

    assert result == events

"""
Tests for the complete Operations Center composition root.

Validates that provider health ingestion and dashboard
provider intelligence share the same health monitor.

The composition root is implemented in:

    operations_center_dashboard_composition.py
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_api import (
    OperationsCenterDashboardAPI,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_composition,
    create_operations_center_dashboard_api,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
)


def create_observation(
    *,
    observed_at: datetime,
    consecutive_failures: int,
) -> ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Create deterministic provider health evidence.
    """

    return ProviderHealthObservation(
        provider="openai",
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
        observed_at=observed_at,
        latency_ms=5000,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        consecutive_successes=0,
        error_type="provider_unavailable",
        error_message="Provider health probe failed.",
    )


def test_complete_composition_creates_dashboard_api() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the complete composition creates a usable
    dashboard API.
    """

    composition = (
        create_operations_center_composition()
    )

    assert isinstance(
        composition.dashboard_api,
        OperationsCenterDashboardAPI,
    )


def test_health_application_service_is_composed() -> None:
    """
    Validate that provider health ingestion is part of the
    same application composition.
    """

    composition = (
        create_operations_center_composition()
    )

    assert (
        composition.health_application_service
        is not None
    )


def test_health_observation_flows_through_shared_composition() -> None:
    """
    Validate that health ingestion and dashboard provider
    intelligence use the same composed health state.
    """

    composition = (
        create_operations_center_composition()
    )

    timestamps = (
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            0,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            1,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            2,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    composition.health_application_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=timestamps[0],
            consecutive_failures=1,
        )
    )

    composition.health_application_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=timestamps[1],
            consecutive_failures=2,
        )
    )

    result = (
        composition.health_application_service.observe(
            create_observation(
                observed_at=timestamps[2],
                consecutive_failures=3,
            )
        )
    )

    assert result.incident is not None

    dashboard = (
        composition.dashboard_api
        .get_dashboard()
    )

    providers = dashboard[
        "active_providers"
    ]

    assert len(providers) == 1

    assert (
        providers[0]["provider"]
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        providers[0]["model"]
        == "gpt-5-mini"
    )


def test_dashboard_api_backward_compatibility() -> None:
    """
    Validate the existing dashboard composition entry point.
    """

    api = (
        create_operations_center_dashboard_api()
    )

    assert isinstance(
        api,
        OperationsCenterDashboardAPI,
    )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center dashboard API tests.

Validates the thin API adapter boundary.

The API adapter must:

- delegate to the dashboard facade;
- expose the facade result unchanged;
- reject invalid provider input;
- reject a missing facade dependency;
- avoid introducing business logic;
- remain read-only.
"""

from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_facade import (
    OperationsCenterDashboardFacade,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_service import (
    ActiveProvider,
)

from app.interfaces.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_api import (
    OperationsCenterDashboardAPI,
)


def create_active_providers() -> tuple[
    ActiveProvider,
    ...
]:
    """
    Create deterministic provider data.
    """

    return (
        ActiveProvider(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capabilities=(
                "conversation",
                "reasoning",
            ),
        ),
        ActiveProvider(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capabilities=(
                "summarisation",
            ),
        ),
    )


def create_facade_mock() -> Mock:
    """
    Create a facade test double.
    """

    facade = Mock(
        spec=OperationsCenterDashboardFacade
    )

    facade.get_dashboard.return_value = {
        "system_confidence": 85.0,
        "monitoring_status": "incident",
        "active_providers": [
            {
                "provider": "openai",
                "model": "gpt-5-mini",
                "capabilities": [
                    "conversation",
                    "reasoning",
                ],
                "active": True,
            }
        ],
        "active_escalations": 1,
        "pending_approvals": 1,
        "approved_escalations": 0,
        "rejected_escalations": 0,
        "pending_notifications": 0,
        "total_escalations": 1,
    }

    return facade


def test_api_delegates_to_facade() -> None:
    """
    Validate that dashboard generation is delegated to the
    application facade.
    """

    facade = create_facade_mock()

    api = OperationsCenterDashboardAPI(
        facade=facade
    )

    providers = create_active_providers()

    result = api.get_dashboard(
        active_providers=providers
    )

    facade.get_dashboard.assert_called_once_with(
        active_providers=providers,
    )

    assert result == (
        facade.get_dashboard.return_value
    )


def test_api_returns_facade_result_unchanged() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the API adapter does not transform or
    alter the application contract.
    """

    facade = create_facade_mock()

    expected = {
        "system_confidence": 100.0,
        "monitoring_status": "healthy",
        "active_providers": [],
        "active_escalations": 0,
        "pending_approvals": 0,
        "approved_escalations": 0,
        "rejected_escalations": 0,
        "pending_notifications": 0,
        "total_escalations": 0,
    }

    facade.get_dashboard.return_value = (
        expected
    )

    api = OperationsCenterDashboardAPI(
        facade=facade
    )

    result = api.get_dashboard(
        active_providers=()
    )

    assert result is expected


def test_api_rejects_none_active_providers() -> None:
    """
    Validate required provider input.
    """

    facade = create_facade_mock()

    api = OperationsCenterDashboardAPI(
        facade=facade
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Active providers cannot be None.",
    ):
        api.get_dashboard(
            active_providers=None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )

    facade.get_dashboard.assert_not_called()


def test_api_rejects_none_facade() -> None:
    """
    Validate dependency injection.
    """

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Operations Center dashboard facade "
            "cannot be None."
        ),
    ):
        OperationsCenterDashboardAPI(
            facade=None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )


def test_api_does_not_add_business_logic() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the API simply exposes the facade contract.

    The API must not calculate confidence, classify incidents,
    or interpret escalation state itself.
    """

    facade = create_facade_mock()

    facade.get_dashboard.return_value = {
        "system_confidence": 55.0,
        "monitoring_status": "incident",
        "active_providers": [],
        "active_escalations": 3,
        "pending_approvals": 2,
        "approved_escalations": 1,
        "rejected_escalations": 1,
        "pending_notifications": 1,
        "total_escalations": 5,
    }

    api = OperationsCenterDashboardAPI(
        facade=facade
    )

    result = api.get_dashboard(
        active_providers=()
    )

    assert result[
        "system_confidence"
    ] == 55.0

    assert result[
        "active_escalations"
    ] == 3

    assert result[
        "pending_approvals"
    ] == 2

    assert result[
        "monitoring_status"
    ] == "incident"

    facade.get_dashboard.assert_called_once()


def test_api_is_read_only() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the API adapter only reads through the
    facade and has no mutation operation.
    """

    facade = create_facade_mock()

    api = OperationsCenterDashboardAPI(
        facade=facade
    )

    result = api.get_dashboard(
        active_providers=()
    )

    assert result is not None

    assert not hasattr(
        api,
        "approve",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        api,
        "reject",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        api,
        "update_provider",
    )

    assert not hasattr(
        api,
        "change_routing",
    )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center dashboard composition tests.

Validates the composition root for the Operations Center.

The composition must assemble:

Provider Health Monitor
        |
        v
Operations Center Provider Service
        |
        v
Dashboard Service
        |
        v
Dashboard Facade
        |
        v
Dashboard API

The composition root must:

- construct the dashboard API successfully;
- preserve dependency boundaries;
- avoid constructing dependencies inside the router;
- expose a usable read-only dashboard API;
- preserve the existing Dashboard API contract.
"""

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_api import (
    OperationsCenterDashboardAPI,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_dashboard_api,
)


def create_empty_dashboard(
    api: OperationsCenterDashboardAPI,
) -> dict:
    """
    Build a deterministic empty dashboard using the current
    Dashboard API contract.

    Provider health integration is intentionally tested in
    a separate vertical slice.
    """

    return api.get_dashboard(
        active_providers=()
    )


def test_dashboard_composition_creates_api() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the composition root creates the dashboard
    API successfully.
    """

    api = (
        create_operations_center_dashboard_api()
    )

    assert isinstance(
        api,
        OperationsCenterDashboardAPI,
    )


def test_dashboard_composition_returns_usable_api() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the composed API can build a dashboard.

    No provider observations have been submitted yet, so the
    explicit provider input is empty.

    This test verifies the composition root without changing
    the existing Dashboard API contract.
    """

    api = (
        create_operations_center_dashboard_api()
    )

    result = create_empty_dashboard(
        api
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        dict,
    )

    assert (
        result["system_confidence"]
        == 100.0
    )

    assert (
        result["monitoring_status"]
        == "healthy"
    )

    assert (
        result["active_providers"]
        == []
    )

    assert (
        result["active_escalations"]
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        result["pending_approvals"]
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        result["approved_escalations"]
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        result["rejected_escalations"]
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        result["pending_notifications"]
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        result["total_escalations"]
        == 0
    )


def test_dashboard_composition_is_read_only() -> None:
    """
    Validate that composing the dashboard does not itself
    perform provider operations or create operational state.
    """

    first_api = (
        create_operations_center_dashboard_api()
    )

    second_api = (
        create_operations_center_dashboard_api()
    )

    first_result = (
        create_empty_dashboard(
            first_api
        )
    )

    second_result = (
        create_empty_dashboard(
            second_api
        )
    )

    assert (
        first_result
        == second_result
    )


def test_dashboard_composition_creates_independent_instances() -> None:
    """
    Validate that composition creates independent application
    instances rather than sharing mutable state accidentally.
    """

    first_api = (
        create_operations_center_dashboard_api()
    )

    second_api = (
        create_operations_center_dashboard_api()
    )

    assert (
        first_api is not second_api
    )

)

def create_workflow(
    *,
    escalation_id: str,
    status: EscalationWorkflowStatus,
    minute: int,
) -> EscalationWorkflow:
    """

        escalation_id=escalation_id,

def test_facade_result_is_json_safe() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the complete dashboard result consists
    only of JSON-compatible primitive/container values.

    This deliberately avoids importing a JSON framework so
    the application layer remains infrastructure-independent.
    """

    facade, repository = create_service()

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .AWAITING_APPROVAL
            ),
            minute=30,
        )
    )

    result = facade.get_dashboard(
        active_providers=(
            create_active_providers()
        )
    )

    def assert_json_safe(
        value: object,
    ) -> None:
        if value is None:
            return

        if isinstance(
            value,
            (
                str,
                int,
                float,
                bool,
            ),
        ):
            return

        if isinstance(value, list):
            for item in value:
                assert_json_safe(item)
            return

        if isinstance(value, dict):
            for key, item in value.items():
                assert isinstance(
                    key,
                    str,
                )
                assert_json_safe(item)
            return

        raise AssertionError(
            "Dashboard contains a non-JSON-safe "
            f"value: {type(value)!r}"
        )

    assert_json_safe(result)


def test_facade_does_not_expose_internal_workflow_objects() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the presentation boundary returns plain
    dictionaries rather than internal workflow objects.
    """

    facade, repository = create_service()

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .AWAITING_APPROVAL
            ),
            minute=30,
        )
    )

    result = facade.get_dashboard(
        active_providers=()
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        dict,
    )

    assert all(
        not isinstance(
            value,
            EscalationWorkflow,
        )
        for value in result.values()
    )


def test_facade_is_read_only() -> None:
    """
    Validate that generating the dashboard does not modify
    repository state.
    """

    facade, repository = create_service()

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .AWAITING_APPROVAL
            ),
            minute=30,
        )
    )

    before = repository.get_all()

    facade.get_dashboard(
        active_providers=(
            create_active_providers()
        )
    )

    after = repository.get_all()

    assert before == after

        match=(

            active_providers=None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\application\provider_management\test_operations_center_dashboard_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center dashboard service tests.

Validates the read-only dashboard contract used by the
Operations Center.

Coverage:

- system confidence;
- monitoring status;
- active providers;
- active escalations;
- pending approvals;
- approved escalations;
- rejected escalations;
- pending notifications;
- total escalation counts.

The dashboard service must not modify workflow state.

Confidence model:

- 100 when there are no escalations;
- subtract 15 points per active escalation;
- pending approval is informational and is not
  penalised a second time;
- confidence cannot fall below 0.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_history_service import (
    EscalationHistoryService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_workflow_repository import (
    InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_workflow_service import (
    EscalationWorkflow,
    EscalationWorkflowStatus,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_service import (
    ActiveProvider,
    MonitoringStatus,
    OperationsCenterDashboardService,
)


def create_workflow(
    *,
    escalation_id: str,
    status: EscalationWorkflowStatus,
    minute: int,
) -> EscalationWorkflow:
    """
    Create deterministic workflow data.
    """

    return EscalationWorkflow(
        escalation_id=escalation_id,
        status=status,
        created_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            minute,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        notification_recipient=(
            "operations@example.com"
            if status
            != EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
            else None
        ),
        notification_subject=(
            "EasyChatBot AI Operations Escalation"
            if status
            != EscalationWorkflowStatus
            .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
            else None
        ),
        approved_at=(
            datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                14,
                15,
                minute,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            )
            if status
            == EscalationWorkflowStatus.APPROVED
            else None
        ),
        rejected_at=(
            datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                14,
                15,
                minute,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            )
            if status
            == EscalationWorkflowStatus.REJECTED
            else None
        ),
        decision_reason=(
            "Operations decision recorded."
            if status
            in (
                EscalationWorkflowStatus.APPROVED,
                EscalationWorkflowStatus.REJECTED,
            )
            else None
        ),
    )


def create_service() -> (
    tuple[
        OperationsCenterDashboardService,
        InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository,
    ]
):
    """
    Create a dashboard service and repository.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository()
    )

    history_service = (
        EscalationHistoryService(
            repository=repository
        )
    )

    dashboard_service = (
        OperationsCenterDashboardService(
            history_service=history_service
        )
    )

    return dashboard_service, repository


def create_active_providers() -> tuple[
    ActiveProvider,
    ...,
]:
    """
    Create deterministic active provider data.
    """

    return (

                "conversation",

        ),

                "summarisation",
            ),
        ),
        ActiveProvider(
            provider="ollama",
            model="gemma4",
            capabilities=(
                "internal_experiments",
            ),
        ),
    )

    )


def test_dashboard_confidence_decreases_with_active_escalation() -> None:
    """
    Validate deterministic operational confidence scoring.

    One active escalation:

        100 - 15 = 85

    Pending approval does not receive an additional
    penalty because it is already represented by the
    active escalation.
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .AWAITING_APPROVAL
            ),
            minute=30,
        )
    )

    dashboard = service.build_dashboard(
        active_providers=()
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.system_confidence
        == 85.0
    )


def test_dashboard_confidence_never_goes_below_zero() -> None:
    """
    Validate confidence lower bound.
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    for index in range(1, 20):
        repository.save(
            create_workflow(
                escalation_id=(
                    f"esc-{index:03d}"
                ),
                status=(
                    EscalationWorkflowStatus
                    .AWAITING_APPROVAL

                    30 + index
                ),
            )
        )

    dashboard = service.build_dashboard(
        active_providers=()
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.system_confidence
        == 0.0
    )

    )

        dashboard.system_confidence
