    )


def test_multiple_workflow_states_are_aggregated() -> None:
    """
    Validate the complete dashboard aggregation.

    Active workflows:

        1 pending notification
        2 awaiting approval

    Therefore:

        active escalations = 3

    Confidence:

        100 - (3 * 15) = 55
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .PENDING_NOTIFICATION
            ),
            minute=30,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-002",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .AWAITING_APPROVAL
            ),
            minute=31,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-003",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .AWAITING_APPROVAL
            ),
            minute=32,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-004",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .APPROVED
            ),
            minute=33,
        )
    )

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-005",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .REJECTED
            ),
            minute=34,
        )
    )

    dashboard = service.build_dashboard(
        active_providers=(
            create_active_providers()
        )
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.pending_notifications
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.pending_approvals
        == 2
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.active_escalations
        == 3
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.approved_escalations
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.rejected_escalations
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.total_escalations
        == 5
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.monitoring_status
        == MonitoringStatus.INCIDENT
    )

    assert (
        dashboard.system_confidence
        == 55.0
    )


def test_none_active_providers_are_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate required provider input.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Active providers cannot be None.",
    ):
        service.build_dashboard(
            active_providers=None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )


def test_dashboard_is_read_only() -> None:
    """
    Validate that dashboard generation does not modify
    persisted workflow state.
    """

    service, repository = create_service()

    repository.save(
        create_workflow(
            escalation_id="esc-001",
            status=(
                EscalationWorkflowStatus
                .AWAITING_APPROVAL
            ),
            minute=30,
        )
    )

    before = repository.get_all()

    service.build_dashboard(
        active_providers=(
            create_active_providers()
        )
    )

    after = repository.get_all()

    assert before == after

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center operational-event composition tests.

Validates the complete composed provider incident path:

Provider Health Observation
        |
        v
Provider Health Application Service
        |
        v
Provider Incident Service
        |
        v
Provider Operational Event Service
        |
        v
Operational Event Service
        |
        v
Operational Event Repository

The test uses the real application composition.

It must not:

- call external providers;
- call external APIs;
- execute Runtime routing;
- perform autonomous remediation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_composition,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEventType,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
)


def create_observation(
    *,
    occurred_at: datetime,
    consecutive_failures: int,
) -> ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Create deterministic provider health evidence.
    """

    return ProviderHealthObservation(
        provider="openai",
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        status=ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE,
        observed_at=occurred_at,
        latency_ms=5000,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        consecutive_successes=0,
        error_type="provider_unavailable",
        error_message="Provider health probe failed.",
    )


def test_composed_provider_incident_creates_operational_event() -> None:
    """
    Validate the complete composed incident-to-event path.
    """

    composition = (
        create_operations_center_composition()
    )

    timestamps = (
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            0,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            1,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            2,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    composition.health_application_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            occurred_at=timestamps[0],
            consecutive_failures=1,
        )
    )

    composition.health_application_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            occurred_at=timestamps[1],
            consecutive_failures=2,
        )
    )

    incident = (
        composition.health_application_service.observe(
            create_observation(
                occurred_at=timestamps[2],
                consecutive_failures=3,
            )
        )
    )

    assert incident.incident is not None

    events = (
        composition.operational_event_service
        .list_events()
    )

    assert len(events) == 1

    event = events[0]

    assert (
        event.event_type
        == OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED
    )

    assert (
        event.correlation_id
        == incident.incident.incident_id
    )

    assert (
        event.metadata["provider"]
        == "openai"
    )


def test_composed_recovery_creates_provider_recovered_event() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the same composed graph records recovery.
    """

    composition = (
        create_operations_center_composition()
    )

    timestamps = (
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            0,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            1,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            2,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            3,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            4,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    composition.health_application_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            occurred_at=timestamps[0],
            consecutive_failures=1,
        )
    )

    composition.health_application_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            occurred_at=timestamps[1],
            consecutive_failures=2,
        )
    )

    incident_result = (
        composition.health_application_service.observe(
            create_observation(
                occurred_at=timestamps[2],
                consecutive_failures=3,
            )
        )
    )

    assert incident_result.incident is not None

    composition.health_application_service.observe(
        ProviderHealthObservation(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            observed_at=timestamps[3],
            latency_ms=100,
            consecutive_failures=0,
            consecutive_successes=1,
            error_type=None,
            error_message=None,
        )
    )

    recovery_result = (
        composition.health_application_service.observe(
            ProviderHealthObservation(
                provider="openai",
                model="gpt-5-mini",
                capability="conversation",
                status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
                observed_at=timestamps[4],
                latency_ms=100,
                consecutive_failures=0,
                consecutive_successes=2,
                error_type=None,
                error_message=None,
            )
        )
    )

    assert recovery_result.incident is None

    events = (
        composition.operational_event_service
        .list_events()
    )

    assert len(events) == 2

    assert (
        events[0].event_type
        == OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED
    )

    assert (
        events[1].event_type
        == OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_RECOVERED
    )

    assert (
        events[1].correlation_id
        == incident_result.incident.incident_id
    )

    """
    Validate that separate composition roots do not share
    in-memory operational event state.
    """

    first = (
        create_operations_center_composition()
    )

    second = (
        create_operations_center_composition()
    )

    assert (
        first.operational_event_service
        .list_events()
        == ()
    )

    assert (
        second.operational_event_service
        .list_events()
        == ()
    )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center operational events API tests.

Validates the read-only application boundary for operational
event history.

The API must:
- require OperationalEventService;
- retrieve events through the application service;
- serialize OperationalEvent objects into stable dictionaries;
- serialize UUIDs and datetimes safely;
- serialize enum values as strings;
- preserve structured metadata;
- preserve event ordering;
- remain read-only.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventSeverity,
    OperationalEventType,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_operational_events_api import (
    OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI,
)


def create_event(
    *,
    event_type: OperationalEventType = (
        OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_HEALTH_CHANGED
    ),
    severity: OperationalEventSeverity = (
        OperationalEventSeverity.INFO
    ),
    minute: int = 0,
    provider: str = "openai",
    correlation_id: str | None = "incident-001",
) -> OperationalEvent:
    """Create deterministic operational event data."""

    return OperationalEvent.create(
        event_type=event_type,
        severity=severity,
        source="provider_management",
        component="ProviderIncidentService",
        occurred_at=datetime(
            2026, 8, 15, 14, minute, tzinfo=timezone.utc
        ),
        message="Operational event test.",
        correlation_id=correlation_id,
        metadata={
            "provider": provider,
            "environment": "test",
        },
    )


def create_service_mock(
    events: tuple[OperationalEvent, ...],
) -> Mock:
    """Create a controlled OperationalEventService test double."""

    service = Mock(spec=OperationalEventService)
    service.list_events.return_value = events
    return service

    """Validate the dependency boundary."""

    with pytest.raises(

            operational_event_service=None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )

    event = create_event()
    service = create_service_mock((event,))

    api = OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI(
        operational_event_service=service,
    )

    api.list_events()

    service.list_events.assert_called_once_with()

    ]

def test_api_does_not_write_events() -> None:
    """Validate the read-only API surface."""

    event = create_event()
    service = create_service_mock((event,))

    api = OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI(
        operational_event_service=service,
    )

    api.list_events()

    service.list_events.assert_called_once_with()

    assert not hasattr(api, "record")
    assert not hasattr(api, "create")
    assert not hasattr(api, "delete")


def test_serialized_event_is_json_safe() -> None:
    """Validate that the response contains JSON-safe values."""

    event = create_event(
        event_type=OperationalEventType.AGENT_EXECUTION_FAILED,
        severity=OperationalEventSeverity.ERROR,
    )
    service = create_service_mock((event,))

    api = OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI(
        operational_event_service=service,
    )

    result = api.list_events()

    def assert_json_safe(value: object) -> None:
        if value is None:
            return

        if isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
            return

        if isinstance(value, list):
            for item in value:
                assert_json_safe(item)
            return

        if isinstance(value, dict):
            for key, item in value.items():
                assert isinstance(key, str)
                assert_json_safe(item)
            return

        raise AssertionError(
            "Response contains a non-JSON-safe "
            f"value: {type(value)!r}"
        )

    assert_json_safe(result)


def test_serialized_event_does_not_expose_internal_objects() -> None:
    """Validate that UUIDs and enums do not cross the API boundary."""

    event = create_event()
    service = create_service_mock((event,))

    api = OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI(
        operational_event_service=service,
    )

    result = api.list_events()

    assert isinstance(result[0]["event_id"], str)
    assert isinstance(result[0]["event_type"], str)
    assert isinstance(result[0]["severity"], str)
    assert not isinstance(
        result[0]["event_type"],
        OperationalEventType,
    )
    assert not isinstance(
        result[0]["severity"],
        OperationalEventSeverity,
    )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider comparison tests.

Validates:

Provider Usage Events
        |
        v
ProviderComparisonService
        |
        v
Provider / Model / Capability Analytics
        |
        v
Capability Optimisation Evidence

The comparison service is analytical only.

It does not:

- change provider routing;
- execute providers;
- approve recommendations;
- send notifications.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.provider_comparison_service import (
    ProviderComparisonService,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import (
    ProviderUsageEvent,
)


def create_event(
    *,
    provider: str,
    model: str,
    capability: str,
    input_tokens: int,
    output_tokens: int,
    latency_ms: int,
    cost: str,
    success: bool = True,
) -> ProviderUsageEvent:
    """
    Create deterministic provider usage data.
    """

    return ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider=provider,
        model=model,
        capability=capability,
        functionality="chat",
        agent="conversation_agent",
        input_tokens=input_tokens,
        output_tokens=output_tokens,
        latency_ms=latency_ms,
        estimated_cost=Decimal(cost),
        quality_score=95.0,
        success=success,
    )


def test_compare_groups_provider_model_and_capability() -> None:
    """
    Validate that comparison analytics keep provider,
    model and capability dimensions separate.
    """

    service = ProviderComparisonService()

    events = [
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            input_tokens=1000,
            output_tokens=500,
            latency_ms=800,
            cost="0.010",
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            input_tokens=1200,
            output_tokens=600,
            latency_ms=1000,
            cost="0.012",
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="conversation",
            input_tokens=900,
            output_tokens=400,
            latency_ms=700,
            cost="0.007",
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="summarisation",
            input_tokens=900,
            output_tokens=300,
            latency_ms=600,
            cost="0.006",
        ),
    ]

    comparisons = service.compare(
        events=events
    )

    assert len(comparisons) == 3

    keys = {
        (
            comparison.provider,
            comparison.model,
            comparison.capability,
        )
        for comparison in comparisons
    }

    assert keys == {
        (
            "openai",
            "gpt-5-mini",
            "conversation",
        ),
        (
            "gemini",
            "gemini-2.5-flash",
            "conversation",
        ),
        (
            "gemini",
            "gemini-2.5-flash",
            "summarisation",
        ),
    }


def test_compare_calculates_operational_metrics() -> None:
    """
    Validate token, latency, success and cost calculations.
    """

    service = ProviderComparisonService()

    events = [
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            input_tokens=1000,
            output_tokens=500,
            latency_ms=800,
            cost="0.010",
            success=True,
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            input_tokens=1200,
            output_tokens=600,
            latency_ms=1000,
            cost="0.012",
            success=False,
        ),
    ]

    comparisons = service.compare(
        events=events
    )

    assert len(comparisons) == 1

    comparison = comparisons[0]

    assert comparison.provider == "openai"

    assert comparison.model == "gpt-5-mini"

    assert comparison.capability == "conversation"

    assert comparison.total_requests == 2

    assert comparison.successful_requests == 1

    assert comparison.success_rate == 50.0

    assert comparison.total_input_tokens == 2200

    assert comparison.total_output_tokens == 1100

    assert comparison.total_tokens == 3300

    assert comparison.average_latency_ms == 900.0

    assert comparison.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0.022"
    )

    assert comparison.average_cost_per_request == (
        Decimal("0.011")
    )


def test_compare_capability_filters_other_capabilities() -> None:
    """
    Validate capability-specific comparison.

    This is critical for adaptive optimisation:
    conversation performance must not be mixed with
    summarisation performance.
    """

    service = ProviderComparisonService()

    events = [
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            input_tokens=1000,
            output_tokens=500,
            latency_ms=800,
            cost="0.010",
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="conversation",
            input_tokens=900,
            output_tokens=400,
            latency_ms=700,
            cost="0.007",
        ),
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="summarisation",
            input_tokens=800,
            output_tokens=300,
            latency_ms=600,
            cost="0.006",
        ),
    ]

    comparisons = (
        service.compare_capability(
            capability="conversation",
            events=events,
        )
    )

    assert len(comparisons) == 2

    assert all(
        comparison.capability
        == "conversation"
        for comparison in comparisons
    )

    providers = {
        comparison.provider
        for comparison in comparisons
    }

    assert providers == {
        "openai",
        "gemini",
    }


def test_empty_events_return_empty_comparison() -> None:
    """
    Validate empty analytical input.
    """

    service = ProviderComparisonService()

    comparisons = service.compare(
        events=[]
    )

    assert comparisons == ()


def test_none_events_are_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate required usage-event input.
    """

    service = ProviderComparisonService()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Usage events cannot be None.",
    ):
        service.compare(
            events=None,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )


def test_empty_capability_is_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate capability filtering input.
    """

    service = ProviderComparisonService()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Capability cannot be empty.",
    ):
        service.compare_capability(
            capability="",
            events=[],
        )

"""
Tests for ProviderHealthApplicationService.

The tests verify that the application service coordinates
the existing provider health monitor and incident service
without creating a second health-state authority.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_application_service import (
    ProviderHealthApplicationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_monitor_service import (
    ProviderHealthMonitorService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_incident_service import (
    InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository,
    ProviderIncidentService,
)


def create_observation(
    *,
    observed_at: datetime,
    consecutive_failures: int,
) -> ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Create deterministic provider health evidence.
    """

    return ProviderHealthObservation(
        provider="openai",
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
        observed_at=observed_at,
        latency_ms=5000,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        consecutive_successes=0,
        error_type="provider_unavailable",
        error_message="Provider health probe failed.",
    )


def create_service() -> (
    ProviderHealthApplicationService
):
    """
    Create the application service using the existing health
    monitor as the single health-state owner.
    """

    health_monitor = (
        ProviderHealthMonitorService()
    )

    incident_repository = (
        InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository()
    )

    incident_service = (
        ProviderIncidentService(
            repository=incident_repository
        )
    )

    return (
        ProviderHealthApplicationService(
            health_monitor=health_monitor,
            incident_service=incident_service,
        )
    )


def test_observation_is_delegated_to_health_monitor() -> None:
    """
    Validate that health processing is delegated to the
    existing ProviderHealthMonitorService.
    """

    service = create_service()

    result = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                15,
                14,
                0,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            ),
            consecutive_failures=1,
        )
    )

    assert (
        result.provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        result.model
        == "gpt-5-mini"
    )

    assert (
        result.capability
        == "conversation"
    )

    assert (
        result.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
    )

    assert result.incident is None


def test_unavailable_transition_creates_incident() -> None:
    """
    Validate the complete health-to-incident application flow.
    """

    service = create_service()

    timestamps = (
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            0,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            1,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            2,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    first = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=timestamps[0],
            consecutive_failures=1,
        )
    )

    second = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=timestamps[1],
            consecutive_failures=2,
        )
    )

    third = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=timestamps[2],
            consecutive_failures=3,
        )
    )

    assert first.incident is None

    assert second.incident is None

    assert (
        third.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    assert (
        third.incident
        is not None
    )

    assert (
        third.incident.provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        third.incident.is_open
        is True
    )

    Validate explicit input validation.

    service = create_service()

        match=(
            "Provider health observation "

        ),
    ):
        service.observe(None)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health escalation tests.

Validates:

Provider Health
        |
        v
Provider Incident
        |
        v
Health Escalation Service
        |
        v
Escalation Service
        |
        v
Provider Escalation

The tests verify that provider availability incidents
are translated into advisory operational escalations.

No email, Runtime routing change, provider execution,
or autonomous action is performed.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_repository import (
    EscalationRepository,
    InMemoryEscalationRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_service import (
    EscalationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationType,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_escalation_service import (
    ProviderHealthEscalationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderIncident,
)


def create_incident(
    *,
    provider: str = "openai",
    status: ProviderHealthStatus = (
        ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
    ),
    consecutive_failures: int = 3,
    capabilities: tuple[str, ...] = (
        "conversation",
    ),
) -> ProviderIncident:
    """
    Create a deterministic provider incident.
    """

    started_at = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        32,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    observed_at = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        35,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    return ProviderIncident(
        incident_id="incident-001",
        provider=provider,
        status=status,
        started_at=started_at,
        last_observed_at=observed_at,
        recovered_at=None,
        affected_capabilities=capabilities,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        resolution_message=None,
    )


def create_service() -> (
    ProviderHealthEscalationService
):
    """
    Create the health escalation service with an
    in-memory escalation repository.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    escalation_service = (
        EscalationService(
            repository=repository
        )
    )

    return ProviderHealthEscalationService(
        escalation_service=escalation_service
    )


def test_unavailable_provider_creates_escalation() -> None:
    """
    Validate that an unavailable provider creates a
    provider failure escalation.
    """

    service = create_service()

    incident = create_incident(
        provider="openai",
        consecutive_failures=3,
        capabilities=(
            "conversation",
        ),
    )

    escalation = service.escalate_incident(
        incident=incident
    )

    assert escalation is not None

    assert (
        escalation.escalation_type
        == EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
    )

    assert (
        escalation.severity
        == EscalationSeverity.HIGH
    )

    assert escalation.provider == "openai"

    assert (
        escalation.capability
        == "conversation"
    )

    assert (
        "currently unavailable"
        in escalation.message
    )

    assert (
        "3"
        in escalation.message
    )

    assert (
        escalation.recommendation
        is not None
    )


def test_multiple_capabilities_are_preserved() -> None:

    service = create_service()

    )

        "conversation, summarisation"
        in escalation.message
    )

        provider="ollama",

            "internal_experiments",
        ),

        incident=incident
    )

        == "ollama"

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health monitor tests.

Validates the vertical flow:

ProviderHealthObservation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        v
ProviderHealthMonitorService
        |
        v
ProviderHealthReadModel

The monitor must:

- delegate health calculation;
- retain latest observation metadata;
- expose provider health read models;
- support multiple providers;
- preserve provider/model/capability attribution;
- track provider failure and recovery;
- remain read-only from an operational perspective.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_monitor_service import (
    ProviderHealthMonitorService,
)


def create_observation(
    *,
    provider: str,
    model: str,
    capability: str,
    status: ProviderHealthStatus,
    observed_at: datetime,
    consecutive_failures: int = 0,
    consecutive_successes: int = 0,
) -> ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Create deterministic provider health evidence.
    """

    return ProviderHealthObservation(
        provider=provider,
        model=model,
        capability=capability,
        status=status,
        observed_at=observed_at,
        latency_ms=700,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        consecutive_successes=consecutive_successes,
    )


def test_observe_returns_health_transition() -> None:

            ),

        )
    )

    assert (
        transition.provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (

    )

def test_unavailable_provider_is_exposed() -> None:
    """
    Validate three consecutive failures produce an
    unavailable provider projection.
    """

    service = ProviderHealthMonitorService()

    timestamps = (
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            10,
            30,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            10,
            31,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            10,
            32,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    for index, timestamp in enumerate(
        timestamps,
        start=1,
    ):
        service.observe(
            create_observation(
                provider="openai",
                model="gpt-5-mini",
                capability="conversation",
                status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
                observed_at=timestamp,
                consecutive_failures=index,
            )
        )

    result = service.get_provider_health(
        "openai"
    )

    assert result is not None

    assert (
        result.status
        == "unavailable"
    )

    assert (
        result.consecutive_failures
        == 3
    )

    assert (
        result.unavailable_since
        == timestamps[-1]
    )


def test_recovery_updates_provider_health() -> None:
    """
    Validate successful recovery updates the read model.

    Provider outage:

        10:32

    Provider recovery:

        10:39

    The current health contract treats the first healthy
    observation as recovery.
    """

    service = ProviderHealthMonitorService()

    recovery_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        15,
        10,
        39,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    failure_timestamps = (
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            10,
            30,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            10,
            31,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            10,
            32,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    failure_transitions = []

    for index, timestamp in enumerate(
        failure_timestamps,
        start=1,
    ):
        failure_transitions.append(
            service.observe(
                create_observation(
                    provider="openai",
                    model="gpt-5-mini",
                    capability="conversation",
                    status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
                    observed_at=timestamp,
                    consecutive_failures=index,
                )
            )
        )

    assert (
        failure_transitions[-1].transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    recovery_transition = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            observed_at=recovery_time,
            consecutive_successes=1,
        )
    )

    assert (
        recovery_transition.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED
    )

    result = service.get_provider_health(
        "openai"
    )

    assert result is not None

    assert (
        result.status
        == "healthy"
    )

    assert (
        result.consecutive_failures
        == 0
    )

    assert (
        result.consecutive_successes
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        result.last_success_at
        == recovery_time
    )

    assert (
        result.unavailable_since
        is None
    )

    assert (
        result.degraded_since
        is None
    )

    """
    Validate provider health isolation.
    """

    service = ProviderHealthMonitorService()

    timestamp = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        15,
        10,
        30,

            observed_at=timestamp,
            consecutive_failures=1,

    openai = service.get_provider_health(

    )

        == "gpt-5-mini"
    )

        gemini.model
        == "gemini-2.5-flash"
    )


def test_get_all_provider_health_is_sorted() -> None:
    """
    Validate deterministic provider ordering.
    """

    service = ProviderHealthMonitorService()

    timestamp = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        15,
        10,
        30,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    service.observe(
        create_observation(
            provider="ollama",
            model="gemma4",
            capability="internal_experiments",
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            observed_at=timestamp,
            consecutive_successes=1,
        )
    )

    service.observe(
        create_observation(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            observed_at=timestamp,
            consecutive_successes=1,
        )
    )

    service.observe(
        create_observation(
            provider="gemini",
            model="gemini-2.5-flash",
            capability="summarisation",
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            observed_at=timestamp,
            consecutive_successes=1,
        )
    )

    results = (
        service.get_all_provider_health()
    )

    assert [
        result.provider
        for result in results
    ] == [
        "gemini",
        "ollama",
        "openai",
    ]

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health read model tests.

Validates the read-only projection between provider health
state and Operations Center presentation data.

The read model must:

- preserve provider identity;
- preserve model identity;
- preserve capability attribution;
- expose calculated health state;
- expose failure/recovery timing;
- reject mismatched provider state;
- remain free of health calculation logic.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthState,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_read_model import (
    ProviderHealthReadModel,
    ProviderHealthReadModelService,
)


def create_observation() -> ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Create deterministic provider health evidence.
    """

    return ProviderHealthObservation(
        provider="openai",
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
        observed_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            10,
            30,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        latency_ms=1800,
        error_type="provider_timeout",
        error_message=(
            "Provider health probe timed out."
        ),
        consecutive_failures=2,
        consecutive_successes=0,
    )


def create_state() -> ProviderHealthState:
    """
    Create deterministic calculated provider state.
    """

    checked_at = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        15,
        10,
        30,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    degraded_since = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        15,
        10,
        29,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    last_failure_at = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        15,
        10,
        30,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    return ProviderHealthState(
        provider="openai",
        status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
        checked_at=checked_at,
        consecutive_failures=2,
        consecutive_successes=0,
        last_success_at=None,
        last_failure_at=last_failure_at,
        degraded_since=degraded_since,
        unavailable_since=None,
    )

        result,

def test_build_rejects_provider_mismatch() -> None:
    """
    Validate that observation and calculated state cannot be
    accidentally combined for different providers.
    """

    service = (
        ProviderHealthReadModelService()
    )

    mismatched_state = ProviderHealthState(
        provider="gemini",
        status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
        checked_at=datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            10,
            30,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        consecutive_failures=0,
        consecutive_successes=2,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Observation provider and health state "
            "provider must match."
        ),
    ):
        service.build(
            observation=create_observation(),
            state=mismatched_state,
        )


def test_read_model_does_not_change_health_state() -> None:
    """
    Validate that building a projection does not mutate the
    calculated health state.
    """

    service = (
        ProviderHealthReadModelService()
    )

    state = create_state()

    result = service.build(
        observation=create_observation(),
        state=state,
    )

    assert (
        state.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        state.consecutive_failures
        == 2
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        == "degraded"
    )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health tests.

Validates:

Provider Health Observation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        +----------------------+
        |                      |
        v                      v
Provider Health State   Health Transition
        |                      |
        +----------+-----------+
                   |
                   v
          Provider Incident
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_service import (
    ProviderHealthService,
)


def create_observation(
    *,
    provider: str = "openai",
    status: ProviderHealthStatus = (
        ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
    ),
    observed_at: datetime | None = None,
    capability: str | None = "conversation",
    consecutive_failures: int = 0,
    consecutive_successes: int = 1,
) -> ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Create deterministic provider health evidence.
    """

    if observed_at is None:
        observed_at = datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            32,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )

    return ProviderHealthObservation(
        provider=provider,
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability=capability,
        status=status,
        observed_at=observed_at,
        latency_ms=800,
        consecutive_failures=(
            consecutive_failures
        ),
        consecutive_successes=(
            consecutive_successes
        ),
    )


def create_service() -> ProviderHealthService:
    """
    Create a fresh health service.
    """

    return ProviderHealthService()


def test_first_failure_creates_degraded_state() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the first failure produces a degraded
    provider state rather than an immediate outage.
    """

    service = create_service()

    event = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
            consecutive_failures=1,
            consecutive_successes=0,
        )
    )

    state = service.get_state("openai")

    assert state is not None

    assert (
        state.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        event.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        event.current_status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        event.consecutive_failures
        == 1
    )


def test_three_consecutive_failures_create_unavailable_state() -> None:
    """
    Validate the outage threshold.

    Three consecutive failures must move the provider
    from degraded to unavailable.
    """

    service = create_service()

    first = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
            consecutive_failures=1,
            consecutive_successes=0,
        )
    )

    second = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
            consecutive_failures=2,
            consecutive_successes=0,
            observed_at=datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                14,
                14,
                33,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            ),
        )
    )

    third = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
            consecutive_failures=3,
            consecutive_successes=0,
            observed_at=datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                14,
                14,
                34,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            ),
        )
    )

    state = service.get_state("openai")

    assert state is not None

    assert (
        first.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        second.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        third.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    assert (
        state.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    assert (
        state.consecutive_failures
        == 3
    )

    assert (
        state.unavailable_since
        == datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            34,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )
    )


def test_successful_observation_recovers_provider() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider recovery.

    A successful observation after an outage returns
    the provider to healthy state and generates a
    recovered transition.
    """

    service = create_service()

    service.observe(
        create_observation(
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
            consecutive_failures=1,
            consecutive_successes=0,
        )
    )

    service.observe(
        create_observation(
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
            consecutive_failures=2,
            consecutive_successes=0,
        )
    )

    service.observe(
        create_observation(
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
            consecutive_failures=3,
            consecutive_successes=0,
        )
    )

    recovery = service.observe(
        create_observation(
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            consecutive_failures=0,
            consecutive_successes=1,
            observed_at=datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                14,
                14,
                35,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            ),
        )
    )

    state = service.get_state("openai")

    assert state is not None

    assert (
        state.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
    )

    assert (
        recovery.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED
    )

    assert (
        recovery.previous_status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    assert (
        recovery.current_status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
    )

    assert (
        state.last_success_at
        == datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            35,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )
    )

    assert (
        state.unavailable_since
        is None
    )


def test_health_state_is_tracked_independently_per_provider() -> None:
    """
    Validate independent provider health tracking.
    """

    service = create_service()

    service.observe(
        create_observation(
            provider="openai",
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
            consecutive_failures=1,
            consecutive_successes=0,
        )
    )

    service.observe(
        create_observation(
            provider="gemini",
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            consecutive_failures=0,
            consecutive_successes=1,
        )
    )

    openai_state = service.get_state(
        "openai"
    )

    gemini_state = service.get_state(
        "gemini"
    )

    assert openai_state is not None
    assert gemini_state is not None

    assert (
        openai_state.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        gemini_state.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
    )

        "ollama",
        "openai",
    ]

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\application\provider_management\test_provider_incident_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider incident tests.

Validates:

Provider Health
        |
        v
Health Transition
        |
        v
Provider Incident Service
        |
        +----------------------+
        |                      |
        v                      v
Open Incident             Recover Incident
        |                      |
        +----------+-----------+
                   |
                   v
             Downtime History

Operational integration:

Provider Incident Service
        |
        +------------------------------+
        |                              |
        v                              v
Incident Repository          Operational Event Service
                                      |
                                      v
                              Operations Center

The incident service does not:

- execute providers;
- change Runtime routing;
- send emails;
- approve recommendations;
- perform autonomous failover.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.provider_operational_event_service import (
    ProviderOperationalEventService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
    ProviderHealthTransitionEvent,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_incident_service import (
    InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository,
    ProviderIncidentService,
)


def create_transition(
    *,
    provider: str = "openai",
    transition: ProviderHealthTransition = (
        ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE
    ),
    occurred_at: datetime | None = None,
    consecutive_failures: int = 3,
    consecutive_successes: int = 0,
    capabilities: tuple[str, ...] = (
        "conversation",
    ),
) -> ProviderHealthTransitionEvent:
    """
    Create a deterministic health transition.
    """

    if occurred_at is None:
        occurred_at = datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            32,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )

    if transition == (
        ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE
    ):
        previous_status = (
            ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
        )
        current_status = (
            ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
        )

    elif transition == (
        ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED
    ):
        previous_status = (
            ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
        )
        current_status = (
            ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
        )

    elif transition == (
        ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
    ):
        previous_status = (
            ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
        )
        current_status = (
            ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
        )

    else:
        previous_status = (
            ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN
        )
        current_status = (
            ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN
        )

    return ProviderHealthTransitionEvent(
        provider=provider,
        transition=transition,
        previous_status=previous_status,
        current_status=current_status,
        occurred_at=occurred_at,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        consecutive_successes=consecutive_successes,
        affected_capabilities=capabilities,
    )


def create_service(
    *,
    operational_event_service: (
        ProviderOperationalEventService | None
    ) = None,
) -> ProviderIncidentService:
    """
    Create an incident service with an in-memory repository.

    The operational event service is optional so that the
    existing incident tests continue to exercise the incident
    service independently from the Operations Center.
    """

    repository = (
        InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository()
    )

    return ProviderIncidentService(
        repository=repository,
        operational_event_service=(
            operational_event_service
        ),
    )


def create_operational_event_mock() -> Mock:
    """
    Create a deterministic operational event service double.
    """

    return Mock(
        spec=ProviderOperationalEventService,
    )


def test_unavailable_transition_creates_incident() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider outage incident creation.
    """

    service = create_service()

    started_at = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        32,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    event = create_transition(
        occurred_at=started_at,
        consecutive_failures=3,
        capabilities=(
            "conversation",
        ),
    )

    incident = service.process_transition(
        event
    )

    assert incident is not None

    assert incident.provider == "openai"

    assert (
        incident.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    assert incident.is_open is True

    assert (
        incident.started_at
        == started_at
    )

    assert (
        incident.last_observed_at
        == started_at
    )

    assert incident.recovered_at is None

    assert (
        incident.affected_capabilities
        == ("conversation",)
    )

    assert (
        incident.consecutive_failures
        == 3
    )

    assert incident.downtime_seconds is None


def test_repeated_unavailable_transitions_update_existing_incident() -> None:
    """
    Validate that repeated failures do not create multiple
    incidents for the same ongoing outage.
    """

    service = create_service()

    started_at = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        32,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    second_observation = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        33,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    first_incident = service.process_transition(
        create_transition(
            occurred_at=started_at,
            consecutive_failures=3,
            capabilities=(
                "conversation",
            ),
        )
    )

    second_incident = service.process_transition(
        create_transition(
            occurred_at=second_observation,
            consecutive_failures=4,
            capabilities=(
                "summarisation",
            ),
        )
    )

    assert first_incident is not None
    assert second_incident is not None

    assert (
        first_incident.incident_id
        == second_incident.incident_id
    )

    assert (
        second_incident.started_at
        == started_at
    )

    assert (
        second_incident.last_observed_at
        == second_observation
    )

    assert (
        second_incident.consecutive_failures
        == 4
    )

    assert (
        second_incident.affected_capabilities
        == (
            "conversation",
            "summarisation",
        )
    )

    incidents = service.list_incidents()

    assert len(incidents) == 1

    """

    )

        == recovered_at
    )

        recovered.last_observed_at
        == recovered_at
    )

    assert (
        recovered.downtime_seconds
        == 420.0
    )

    assert (
        recovered.resolution_message
        is not None
    )

        incident=first_incident,
    )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider operational event tests.

Validates the provider-specific adapter between:

Provider Incident
        |
        v
ProviderOperationalEventService
        |
        v
OperationalEventService
        |
        v
OperationalEvent

The adapter must:

- create PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED events;
- create PROVIDER_RECOVERED events;
- preserve incident correlation;
- preserve provider metadata;
- delegate persistence to OperationalEventService;
- reject invalid input;
- remain read-only with respect to provider state.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from unittest.mock import Mock

import pytest

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventSeverity,
    OperationalEventType,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderIncident,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_operational_event_service import (
    ProviderOperationalEventService,
)


def create_incident(
    *,
    incident_id: str = "incident-001",
    provider: str = "openai",
    status: ProviderHealthStatus = (
        ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
    ),
    started_at: datetime | None = None,
    recovered_at: datetime | None = None,
    capabilities: tuple[str, ...] = (
        "conversation",
    ),
    consecutive_failures: int = 3,
) -> ProviderIncident:
    """
    Create deterministic provider incident data.
    """

    if started_at is None:
        started_at = datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            0,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )

    return ProviderIncident(
        incident_id=incident_id,
        provider=provider,
        status=status,
        started_at=started_at,
        last_observed_at=(
            recovered_at
            if recovered_at is not None
            else started_at
        ),
        recovered_at=recovered_at,
        affected_capabilities=capabilities,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        resolution_message=(
            "Provider recovered."
            if recovered_at is not None
            else None
        ),
    )


def create_service() -> tuple[
    ProviderOperationalEventService,
    Mock,
]:
    """
    Create the provider operational event adapter with a
    controlled generic operational event service.
    """

    operational_event_service = Mock(
        spec=OperationalEventService,
    )

    operational_event_service.record.side_effect = (
        lambda event: event
    )

    service = ProviderOperationalEventService(
        operational_event_service=(
            operational_event_service
        )
    )

    return (
        service,
        operational_event_service,
    )


def test_incident_created_creates_operational_event() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider incident creation mapping.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    incident = create_incident()

    result = service.record_incident_created(
        incident=incident,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        OperationalEvent,
    )

    assert (
        result.event_type
        == OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED
    )

    assert (
        result.severity
        == OperationalEventSeverity.CRITICAL
    )

    assert (
        result.source
        == "provider_management"
    )

    assert (
        result.component
        == "ProviderIncidentService"
    )

    assert (
        result.occurred_at
        == incident.started_at
    )

    assert (
        result.correlation_id
        == incident.incident_id
    )


def test_incident_created_preserves_provider_metadata() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider incident context in structured metadata.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    incident = create_incident(
        provider="gemini",
        incident_id="incident-002",
        capabilities=(
            "summarisation",
            "reasoning",
        ),
        consecutive_failures=5,
    )

    result = service.record_incident_created(
        incident=incident,
    )

    assert (
        result.metadata["provider"]
        == "gemini"
    )

    assert (
        result.metadata["incident_id"]
        == "incident-002"
    )

    assert (
        result.metadata["status"]
        == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE.value
    )

    assert (
        result.metadata["affected_capabilities"]
        == [
            "summarisation",
            "reasoning",
        ]
    )

    assert (
        result.metadata["consecutive_failures"]
        == 5
    )


def test_incident_created_is_persisted_through_generic_service() -> None:
    """
    Validate delegation to the generic operational event service.
    """

    service, operational_events = create_service()

    incident = create_incident()

    result = service.record_incident_created(
        incident=incident,
    )

    operational_events.record.assert_called_once_with(
        result
    )


def test_provider_recovered_creates_operational_event() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider recovery mapping.
    """

    service, _ = create_service()

    recovered_at = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        15,
        14,
        7,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    incident = create_incident(

    )

    result = service.record_provider_recovered(
        incident=incident,
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        OperationalEvent,
    )

    assert (
        result.event_type
        == OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_RECOVERED
    )

    assert (
        result.severity
        == OperationalEventSeverity.INFO
    )

    assert (
        result.source
        == "provider_management"
    )

    assert (
        result.component
        == "ProviderIncidentService"
    )

    assert (
        result.occurred_at
        == recovered_at
    )

    assert (
        result.correlation_id
        == incident.incident_id
    )

"""
AI Service Provider Manager recommendation tests.

Validates:

Provider Intelligence
        |
        v
ProviderRecommendationService
        |
        v
ProviderRecommendation


The recommendation service does not:

- change provider routing;
- execute providers;
- bypass approval;
- modify Runtime configuration.
"""

import pytest


from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    RecommendationStatus,
)

from app.application.provider_management.recommendation_service import (
    ProviderRecommendationService,
)


def test_provider_recommendation_is_created() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider optimisation recommendation.
    """

    service = ProviderRecommendationService()

    recommendation = (
        service.recommend_provider_change(
            capability="research",
            current_provider="openai",
            current_model="gpt-5-mini",
            recommended_provider="gemini",
            recommended_model="gemini-pro",
            reason=(
                "Similar quality with lower "
                "estimated cost."
            ),
            confidence=91.0,
        )
    )

    assert recommendation.capability == (
        "research"
    )

    assert recommendation.current_provider == (
        "openai"
    )

    assert recommendation.recommended_provider == (
        "gemini"
    )

    assert recommendation.confidence == 91.0

    assert recommendation.status == (
        RecommendationStatus.REVIEW_REQUIRED
    )


def test_invalid_confidence_is_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate recommendation safety checks.
    """

    service = ProviderRecommendationService()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Confidence must be between 0 and 100."
        ),
    ):
        service.recommend_provider_change(
            capability="conversation",
            current_provider="openai",
            current_model="gpt-5-mini",
            recommended_provider="ollama",
            recommended_model="local-model",
            reason="Lower infrastructure cost.",
            confidence=120.0,
        )


def test_empty_capability_is_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate required capability field.
    """

    service = ProviderRecommendationService()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(

        ),
    ):
        service.recommend_provider_change(
            capability="",
            current_provider="openai",
            current_model="gpt-5-mini",
            recommended_provider="gemini",
            recommended_model="gemini-pro",
            reason="Alternative provider.",
            confidence=80.0,
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_api_provider_wiring.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
API provider wiring integration tests.

Verifies that the API composition layer creates an
LLMService through the LLMProviderFactory and injects
that service into ConversationAgent.

The real OpenAI API is never called.
"""

import pytest

from backend.api import routes
from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)
from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)
from backend.services.llm_service import (
    LLMService,
)


class FakeProvider:
    """
    Deterministic provider used by the integration test.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        self.received_request = request

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output="Fake AI response",
        )


class FakeConversationAgent:
    """
    Test double for ConversationAgent.

    Captures the injected LLMService so the API
    composition boundary can be verified.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        character,
        llm_service=None,
    ):
        self.character = character
        self.llm_service = llm_service


def create_character():
    """
    Create deterministic test character.
    """

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


def test_create_companion_service_injects_llm_service(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Verify API composition creates an LLMService
    through the provider factory and injects it
    into ConversationAgent.
    """

    captured = {}

    def fake_factory_create(
        provider="openai",
    ):
        fake_provider = FakeProvider()

        captured["provider"] = fake_provider
        captured["provider_name"] = provider

        return fake_provider

    def fake_conversation_agent(
        character,
        llm_service=None,
    ):
        agent = FakeConversationAgent(
            character=character,
            llm_service=llm_service,
        )

        captured["character"] = character
        captured["agent"] = agent
        captured["llm_service"] = llm_service

        return agent

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes.LLMProviderFactory,
        "create",
        staticmethod(
            fake_factory_create
        ),
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        routes,
        "ConversationAgent",
        fake_conversation_agent,
    )

    character = (
        routes.character_registry.get(
            "yeti"
        )
    )

    service = (
        routes.create_companion_service(
            character
        )
    )

    assert service is not None

    assert (
        captured["character"]
        == character
    )

    assert (
        captured["llm_service"]
        is not None
    )

    assert isinstance(
        captured["llm_service"],
        LLMService,
    )

    assert (
        captured["provider"]
        is captured[
            "llm_service"
        ].llm_provider
    )

    assert (
        captured["provider_name"]
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        captured["agent"].llm_service
        is captured["llm_service"]
    )

"""
API provider wiring integration test.

Verifies that the API composition layer injects
the LLM provider into ConversationAgent.

The OpenAI API call is replaced with a fake provider.
"""

import pytest

from backend.api import routes
from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeLLMProvider:
    """
    Fake provider replacing OpenAI.

    Ensures API composition injects an LLM provider
    without calling external services.
    """

    def __init__(self):
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ):
        self.received_request = request

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output=(
                "Fake AI response"
            ),
        )


def create_character():

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_receives_provider():

    provider = FakeLLMProvider()

    from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
        ConversationAgent,
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_provider=provider,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="integration-user",
        request=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence"
        ),
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert (
        result.output["message"]
        == "Fake AI response"
    )

    assert (
        result.output["provider_id"]
        == "fake_llm"
    )

    assert (
        provider.received_request.user_prompt
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center HTTP integration test.

Validates the composed Operations Center through the HTTP
boundary.

Flow:

Provider Health Observation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthApplicationService
        |
        v
Shared Operations Center Composition
        |
        v
Dashboard API
        |
        v
Operations Center Router
        |
        v
HTTP JSON

No external provider calls are executed.

No Runtime routing is changed.

No autonomous operational action is performed.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_composition,
)

from app.interfaces.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_router import (
    configure_dashboard_api,
    router,
)


def create_observation(
    *,
    observed_at: datetime,
    consecutive_failures: int,
):
    """
    Create deterministic provider health evidence.
    """

    from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
        ProviderHealthObservation,
        ProviderHealthStatus,
    )

    return ProviderHealthObservation(
        provider="openai",
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
        observed_at=observed_at,
        latency_ms=5000,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        consecutive_successes=0,
        error_type="provider_unavailable",
        error_message="Provider health probe failed.",
    )


def create_composed_app() -> tuple[
    FastAPI,
    object,
]:
    """
    Create a FastAPI application using the real Operations
    Center composition.
    """

    composition = (
        create_operations_center_composition()
    )

    configure_dashboard_api(
        composition.dashboard_api
    )

    app = FastAPI(
        title="EasyChatBot AI Operations Center"
    )

    app.include_router(router)

    return app, composition


def test_operations_center_dashboard_http_starts_healthy() -> None:
    """
    Validate the real composed dashboard endpoint before
    provider health evidence is submitted.
    """

    app, _ = create_composed_app()

    client = TestClient(app)

    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/dashboard"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert (
        body["monitoring_status"]
        == "healthy"
    )

    assert (
        body["system_confidence"]
        == 100.0
    )

    assert (
        body["active_providers"]
        == []
    )


def test_provider_health_reaches_real_dashboard_http() -> None:
    """
    Validate that provider health evidence reaches the real
    Operations Center HTTP dashboard.
    """

    app, composition = (
        create_composed_app()
    )

    timestamps = (
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            0,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            1,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            15,
            14,
            2,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    composition.health_application_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=timestamps[0],
            consecutive_failures=1,
        )
    )

    composition.health_application_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=timestamps[1],
            consecutive_failures=2,
        )
    )

    result = (
        composition.health_application_service.observe(
            create_observation(
                observed_at=timestamps[2],
                consecutive_failures=3,
            )
        )
    )

    assert result.incident is not None

    client = TestClient(app)

    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/dashboard"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    providers = body[
        "active_providers"
    ]

    assert len(providers) == 1

    assert (
        providers[0]["provider"]
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        providers[0]["model"]
        == "gpt-5-mini"
    )


def test_operations_center_dashboard_http_is_read_only() -> None:
    """
    Validate that the dashboard HTTP boundary does not expose
    mutation methods.
    """

    app, _ = create_composed_app()

    client = TestClient(app)

    response = client.post(
        "/api/operations-center/dashboard"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 405
