"""
Integration test for the real Parallel Search Provider.

Sprint H1.8 â€” Real Provider Validation

This test validates:

Application
    â†“
SearchProvider interface
    â†“
ParallelSearchProvider
    â†“
Parallel API
    â†“
Normalised SearchResult

The test is intentionally isolated from:
- Conversation Agent
- Challenge Engine
- Live Intelligence Agent

The test is skipped when PARALLEL_API_KEY is not configured.
"""

import os

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)
from src.providers.search_provider.parallel_adapter import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_search_provider_returns_normalised_results():
    """
    Validate that the real Parallel provider returns
    normalised SearchResult objects.
    """

    api_key = os.getenv("PARALLEL_API_KEY")

    if not api_key:
        pytest.skip(
            "PARALLEL_API_KEY is not configured"
        )

    provider = ParallelSearchProvider(
        api_key=api_key,
    )

    results = await provider.search(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        max_results=3,
    )

    assert isinstance(results, list)

    assert len(results) > 0

    for result in results:
        assert isinstance(result, SearchResult)

        assert result.title.strip()
        assert result.url.strip()
        assert result.source.strip()

        # Content may legitimately be empty depending
        # on the provider response, so it is not required
        # for the provider contract test.

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_provider_health_to_escalation.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health vertical slice.

Validates the complete internal operational flow:

Health Observation
        |
        v
Provider Health Service
        |
        v
UNAVAILABLE
        |
        v
Provider Incident Service
        |
        v
Provider Incident
        |
        v
Health Escalation Service
        |
        v
Escalation Service
        |
        v
Escalation Repository

No external provider calls are executed.

No email is sent.

No Runtime routing is changed.

No autonomous operational action is performed.
"""

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_repository import (
    InMemoryEscalationRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_service import (
    EscalationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationType,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_escalation_service import (
    ProviderHealthEscalationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_service import (
    ProviderHealthService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_incident_service import (
    InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository,
    ProviderIncidentService,
)


def create_observation(
    *,
    observed_at: datetime,
    consecutive_failures: int,
) -> ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Create deterministic OpenAI health evidence.
    """

    return ProviderHealthObservation(
        provider="openai",
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        status=ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED,
        observed_at=observed_at,
        latency_ms=5000,
        consecutive_failures=consecutive_failures,
        consecutive_successes=0,
        error_type="provider_unavailable",
        error_message="Provider health probe failed.",
    )


def test_provider_health_to_escalation_vertical_slice() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider failure detection through escalation
    persistence.
    """

    health_service = ProviderHealthService()

    incident_repository = (
        InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository()
    )

    incident_service = (
        ProviderIncidentService(
            repository=incident_repository
        )
    )

    escalation_repository = (
        InMemoryEscalationRepository()
    )

    escalation_service = (
        EscalationService(
            repository=escalation_repository
        )
    )

    health_escalation_service = (
        ProviderHealthEscalationService(
            escalation_service=escalation_service
        )
    )

    first_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        32,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    second_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        33,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    third_time = datetime(
        2026,
        8,
        14,
        14,
        34,
        tzinfo=timezone.utc,
    )

    first_transition = health_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=first_time,
            consecutive_failures=1,
        )
    )

    second_transition = health_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=second_time,
            consecutive_failures=2,
        )
    )

    third_transition = health_service.observe(
        create_observation(
            observed_at=third_time,
            consecutive_failures=3,
        )
    )

    assert (
        first_transition.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        second_transition.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED
    )

    assert (
        third_transition.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    incident = (
        incident_service.process_transition(
            third_transition
        )
    )

    assert incident is not None

    assert (
        incident.provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        incident.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
    )

    assert incident.is_open is True

    assert (
        incident.started_at
        == third_time
    )

    assert (
        incident.affected_capabilities
        == ("conversation",)
    )

    escalation = (
        health_escalation_service
        .escalate_incident(
            incident=incident
        )
    )

    assert escalation is not None

    assert (
        escalation.escalation_type
        == EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
    )

    assert (
        escalation.severity
        == EscalationSeverity.HIGH
    )

    assert (
        escalation.provider
        == "openai"
    )

    assert (
        escalation.capability
        == "conversation"
    )

    assert (
        "currently unavailable"
        in escalation.message
    )

    assert (
        escalation.recommendation
        is not None
    )

    assert (
        "human"
        in escalation.recommendation.lower()
    )

    persisted_escalations = (
        escalation_service
        .list_escalations()
    )

    assert (
        len(persisted_escalations)
        == 1
    )

    assert (
        persisted_escalations[0]
        .escalation_id
        == escalation.escalation_id
    )


def test_provider_recovery_closes_incident() -> None:
    """
    Validate the recovery side of the vertical slice.

    Provider degraded:

        14:32

    Provider degraded:

        14:33

    Provider becomes unavailable:

        14:34

    Provider recovery:

        14:39

    Expected confirmed downtime:

        300 seconds

    The incident starts when the provider crosses the
    confirmed UNAVAILABLE threshold at 14:34.
    """

    health_service = ProviderHealthService()

    incident_repository = (
        InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository()
    )

    incident_service = (
        ProviderIncidentService(
            repository=incident_repository
        )
    )

    failure_times = [
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            32,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            33,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
        datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            34,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    ]

    failure_events = []

    for index, timestamp in enumerate(
        failure_times,
        start=1,
    ):
        failure_events.append(
            health_service.observe(
                create_observation(
                    observed_at=timestamp,
                    consecutive_failures=index,
                )
            )
        )

    incident = (
        incident_service.process_transition(
            failure_events[-1]
        )
    )

    assert incident is not None

    assert (
        incident.started_at
        == datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            34,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )
    )

    recovery_first = health_service.observe(
        ProviderHealthObservation(
            provider="openai",
            model="gpt-5-mini",
            capability="conversation",
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
            observed_at=datetime(
                2026,
                8,
                14,
                14,
                39,
                tzinfo=timezone.utc,
            ),
            latency_ms=700,
            consecutive_failures=0,
            consecutive_successes=1,
        )
    )

    assert (
        recovery_first.transition
        == ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED
    )

    recovered = (
        incident_service.process_transition(
            recovery_first
        )
    )

    assert recovered is not None

    assert recovered.is_open is False

    assert (
        recovered.status
        == ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
    )

    assert (
        recovered.downtime_seconds
        == 300.0
    )

    assert (
        recovered.recovered_at
        == datetime(
            2026,
            8,
            14,
            14,
            39,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        )
    )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\integration\test_provider_usage_vertical_slice.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
AI Runtime provider intelligence vertical slice.

Validates:

Capability Request
        |
        v
Runtime Execution
        |
        v
Usage Capture
        |
        v
Provider Analytics

No real external provider calls are executed.

This test validates the internal intelligence
boundary only.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import (
    ProviderUsageEvent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.repository import (
    InMemoryProviderUsageRepository,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.service import (
    ProviderUsageService,
)


def test_provider_usage_vertical_slice() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider usage tracking lifecycle.

    The usage service is responsible for:

    - recording execution consumption;
    - aggregating provider information;
    - exposing analytics data.

    It does not:

    - call providers;
    - execute agents;
    - perform payments.

    Persistence is provided through the
    repository boundary.
    """

    usage_service = ProviderUsageService(
        repository=(
            InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
        ),
    )

    event = ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider="openai",

        model="gpt-5-mini",

        capability="conversation",

        functionality="chat",

        agent="conversation_agent",

        input_tokens=1000,

        output_tokens=500,

        latency_ms=800,

        estimated_cost=Decimal(
            "0.010"
        ),

        quality_score=95.0,

        success=True,
    )

    usage_service.record(
        event
    )

    report = usage_service.summary()

    assert report.total_requests == 1

    assert report.providers == (
        "openai",
    )

    assert report.total_tokens == 1500

    assert report.successful_requests == 1

    assert report.failed_requests == 0

    assert report.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0.010"
    )

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

def create_test_dashboard_api() -> OperationsCenterDashboardAPI:
    """Create a deterministic dashboard API test double."""

    class TestDashboardAPI:
        def get_dashboard(self) -> dict:
            return {
                "system_confidence": 92.0,
                "monitoring_status": "healthy",
                "active_providers": [
                    {
                        "provider": "openai",
                        "model": "gpt-5-mini",
                        "capabilities": [
                            "conversation",
                            "reasoning",
                        ],
                        "active": True,
                    },
                    {
                        "provider": "gemini",
                        "model": "gemini-2.5-flash",
                        "capabilities": ["summarisation"],
                        "active": True,
                    },
                ],
                "active_escalations": 0,
                "pending_approvals": 0,
                "approved_escalations": 0,
                "rejected_escalations": 0,
                "pending_notifications": 0,
                "total_escalations": 0,
            }

    return TestDashboardAPI()  # type: ignore[return-value]


def create_test_operational_events_api() -> (
    OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI
):
    """Create a deterministic operational-events API test double."""

    class TestOperationalEventsAPI:
        def list_events(self) -> list[dict]:
            return [
                {
                    "event_id": (
                        "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001"
                    ),
                    "event_type": "provider_incident_created",
                    "severity": "critical",
                    "source": "provider_management",
                    "component": "ProviderIncidentService",
                    "occurred_at": "2026-08-15T14:00:00+00:00",
                    "message": "Provider incident created.",
                    "correlation_id": "incident-001",
                    "metadata": {"provider": "openai"},
                }
            ]

    return TestOperationalEventsAPI()  # type: ignore[return-value]


def create_test_app() -> FastAPI:
    """Create a minimal FastAPI application with the router."""

    app = FastAPI(title="EasyChatBot AI Operations Center")
    app.include_router(router)

    app.dependency_overrides[get_dashboard_api] = (
        create_test_dashboard_api

    app.dependency_overrides[get_operational_events_api] = (
        create_test_operational_events_api
    )

    return app

    response = TestClient(app).get(
        "/api/operations-center/dashboard"
    )
    assert response.status_code == 200

"""
Provider cost service unit tests.

Validates:

CostCalculationInput
        |
        v
ProviderCostService
        |
        v
ProviderCostResult

The service does not:

- call providers;
- access billing APIs;
- persist costs;
- render dashboards.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

from src.ai_runtime.providers.cost.models import (
    CostCalculationInput,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.cost.service import (
    ProviderCostService,
)


def create_usage_input() -> CostCalculationInput:
    """
    Create deterministic usage input.
    """

    return CostCalculationInput(
        provider="openai",
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        input_tokens=1000,
        output_tokens=500,
    )


def test_calculate_provider_cost() -> None:
    """
    Validate provider cost calculation.
    """

    service = ProviderCostService()

    result = service.calculate(
        usage=create_usage_input(),

        input_price_per_million_tokens=(
            Decimal("1.00")
        ),

        output_price_per_million_tokens=(
            Decimal("5.00")
        ),
    )

    assert result.provider == "openai"

    assert result.model == "gpt-5-mini"

    assert result.input_tokens == 1000

    assert result.output_tokens == 500

    assert result.input_cost == Decimal(
        "0.001"
    )

    assert result.output_cost == Decimal(
        "0.0025"
    )

    assert result.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0.0035"
    )


def test_zero_tokens_generate_zero_cost() -> None:
    """
    Validate zero token usage.
    """

    service = ProviderCostService()

    result = service.calculate(
        usage=CostCalculationInput(
            provider="ollama",
            model="local-model",
            input_tokens=0,
            output_tokens=0,
        ),

        input_price_per_million_tokens=(
            Decimal("1.00")
        ),

        output_price_per_million_tokens=(
            Decimal("5.00")
        ),
    )

    assert result.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0"
    )


def test_negative_pricing_is_rejected() -> None:
    """
    Validate pricing protection.
    """

    service = ProviderCostService()

    try:
        service.calculate(
            usage=create_usage_input(),

            input_price_per_million_tokens=(
                Decimal("-1.00")
            ),

            output_price_per_million_tokens=(
                Decimal("5.00")
            ),
        )

        assert False, (
            "Negative pricing should fail."
        )

    except ValueError as error:

        assert str(error) == (
            "Input token price cannot be negative."
        )

"""
Tests for Search Provider Interface.

Validates:
- SearchResult data model behaviour
- SearchProvider abstract contract
"""

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchProvider,
    SearchResult,
)


def test_search_result_creation():
    """
    SearchResult should store normalised provider output.
    """

    result = SearchResult(
        title="AI Act Update",
        url="https://example.com/article",
        content="Latest AI regulation news.",
        source="example",
    )

    assert result.title == "AI Act Update"
    assert result.url == "https://example.com/article"
    assert result.content == "Latest AI regulation news."
    assert result.source == "example"


def test_search_result_is_immutable():
    """
    SearchResult is frozen and should not allow mutation.
    """

    result = SearchResult(
        title="Test",
        url="https://example.com",
        content="Content",
        source="test",
    )

    with pytest.raises(AttributeError):
        result.title = "Changed"


def test_search_provider_cannot_be_instantiated():
    """
    SearchProvider is an abstract interface.
    """

    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        SearchProvider()

"""
Integration test for the real Parallel Search Provider.

Sprint H1.8 â€” Real Provider Validation

Flow:

SearchProvider Interface
        â†“
ParallelSearchProvider
        â†“
Existing ParallelClientWrapper
        â†“
Parallel SDK
        â†“
Parallel Search API

The API key is loaded by the existing ParallelClientWrapper
from the project's .env configuration.
"""

import pytest

from src.providers.search_provider.interface import (
    SearchResult,
)

from src.providers.search_provider.parallel_adapter import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_search_provider_returns_normalised_results():
    """
    Validate the real Parallel provider.
    """

    try:
        provider = ParallelSearchProvider()

    except ValueError as exc:
        pytest.skip(
            f"Parallel API credentials are not configured: {exc}"
        )

    results = await provider.search(
        query="latest European AI regulation",
        max_results=3,
    )

    assert isinstance(results, list)

    assert len(results) > 0

    for result in results:
        assert isinstance(
            result,
            SearchResult,
        )

        assert result.title.strip()
        assert result.url.strip()
        assert result.source == "parallel"

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\providers\test_openai_provider.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
Tests for OpenAIProvider.

These tests never call the real OpenAI API.

The OpenAI AsyncOpenAI client is injected with a fake client
so provider behaviour can be tested deterministically.
"""

from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from backend.conversation.conversation_request import (
    ConversationRequest,
)

from backend.providers.llm.openai_provider import (
    OpenAIProvider,
)


class FakeResponses:
    """Fake OpenAI Responses API."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        response=None,
        error=None,
    ):
        self.response = response
        self.error = error
        self.received_kwargs = None

    async def create(
        self,
        **kwargs,
    ):
        self.received_kwargs = kwargs

        if self.error is not None:
            raise self.error

        return self.response


class FakeOpenAIClient:
    """Fake AsyncOpenAI client."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        response=None,
        error=None,
    ):
        self.responses = FakeResponses(
            response=response,
            error=error,
        )


def create_request(
    *,
    context=None,
):
    """Create a valid conversation request."""

    return ConversationRequest(
        system_instruction=(
            "You are Yeti, a friendly AI companion."
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
        conversation_context=context,
        character_id="yeti",
        character_name="Yeti",
    )


def test_openai_provider_initialises():
    """Provider should initialise with a valid model."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient()

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    assert provider.provider_id == "openai"
    assert provider.model == "gpt-5-mini"


def test_openai_provider_rejects_empty_model():
    """Provider should reject an empty model identifier."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="OpenAI model cannot be empty",
    ):
        OpenAIProvider(
            model="",
            client=client,
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_generates_response():
    """Successful OpenAI response should become ProviderResult."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="Artificial intelligence is the field of AI.",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is True
    assert result.provider_id == "openai"
    assert (
        result.output
        == "Artificial intelligence is the field of AI."
    )
    assert result.error is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_builds_correct_request():
    """Provider should correctly translate ConversationRequest."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="Test response.",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    request = create_request(
        context=(
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE\n"
            "Research query: AI regulation"
        ),
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        request,
    )

    assert result.success is True

    sent = (
        client.responses.received_kwargs
    )

    assert sent is not None

    assert sent["model"] == "gpt-5-mini"

    messages = sent["input"]

    assert messages[0] == {
        "role": "system",
        "content": (
            "You are Yeti, a friendly AI companion."
        ),
    }

    assert messages[1] == {
        "role": "user",
        "content": (
            "Additional context:\n"
            "LIVE INTELLIGENCE\n"
            "Research query: AI regulation"
        ),
    }

    assert messages[2] == {
        "role": "user",
        "content": (
            "Explain artificial intelligence."
        ),
    }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_without_context():
    """Provider should not create a context message when absent."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="Test response.",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is True

    messages = (
        client.responses.received_kwargs["input"]
    )

    assert len(messages) == 2

    assert messages[0]["role"] == "system"
    assert messages[1]["role"] == "user"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_handles_api_error():
    """OpenAI exceptions should become failed ProviderResult."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        error=RuntimeError(
            "OpenAI service unavailable"
        ),
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is False
    assert result.provider_id == "openai"
    assert result.output is None
    assert (
        result.error
        == "OpenAI service unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_handles_empty_response():
    """Empty OpenAI output should produce a failed result."""

    response = SimpleNamespace(
        output_text="",
    )

    client = FakeOpenAIClient(
        response=response,
    )

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    result = await provider.generate(
        create_request(),
    )

    assert result.success is False
    assert result.provider_id == "openai"
    assert result.output is None
    assert (
        result.error
        == "OpenAI returned an empty response."
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_openai_provider_rejects_none_request():
    """Provider should reject a missing conversation request."""

    client = FakeOpenAIClient()

    provider = OpenAIProvider(
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        client=client,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Conversation request cannot be None",
    ):
        await provider.generate(None)

"""
Parallel Provider Tests.

These tests validate the provider layer
without calling the external Parallel API.

The real API integration is demonstrated
through the hackathon demo flow.
"""

import pytest

from backend.providers.parallel.parallel_provider import (
    ParallelSearchProvider,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class MockParallelClient:
    """
    Fake Parallel client for automated tests.
    """

    async def search(
        self,
        query: str,
    ):

        return {
            "query": query,
            "results": [
                {
                    "title": "Example source",
                    "url": "https://example.com",
                }
            ],
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_parallel_provider_returns_results(
    monkeypatch,
):

    provider = ParallelSearchProvider(
    client=MockParallelClient()
)

    provider.client = MockParallelClient()

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Latest AI news",
    )

    result = await provider.execute(
        context
    )

    assert isinstance(
        result,
        ProviderResult,
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.provider_id == (
        "parallel_search"
    )

    assert result.output["query"] == (
        "Latest AI news"
    )

    assert len(
        result.output["results"]
    ) == 1

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\providers\usage\test_provider_usage_service.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
Provider usage service unit tests.

Validates:

ProviderUsageEvent
        |
        v
ProviderUsageRepository
        |
        v
ProviderUsageService
        |
        v
ProviderUsageSummary

The service does not:

- call providers;
- access external APIs;
- persist directly;
- execute agents.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.models import (
    ProviderUsageEvent,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.repository import (
    InMemoryProviderUsageRepository,
)

from src.ai_runtime.providers.usage.service import (
    ProviderUsageService,
)


def create_service() -> ProviderUsageService:
    """
    Create service with injected repository.
    """

    return ProviderUsageService(
        repository=(
            InMemoryProviderUsageRepository()
        ),
    )


def create_event(
    *,
    provider: str = "openai",
    success: bool = True,
) -> ProviderUsageEvent:
    """
    Create deterministic usage event data.
    """

    return ProviderUsageEvent(
        provider=provider,
        model="gpt-5-mini",
        capability="conversation",
        functionality="chat",
        agent="conversation_agent",
        input_tokens=1000,
        output_tokens=500,
        latency_ms=800,
        estimated_cost=Decimal(
            "0.010"
        ),
        quality_score=95.0,
        success=success,
    )


def test_record_single_usage_event() -> None:
    service = create_service()

    service.record(
        create_event()
    )

    summary = service.summary()

    assert summary.total_requests == 1

    assert summary.total_tokens == 1500


def test_multiple_providers_are_aggregated() -> None:
    service = create_service()

    service.record(
        create_event(
            provider="openai",
        )
    )

    service.record(
        create_event(
            provider="gemini",
        )
    )

    summary = service.summary()

    assert summary.total_requests == 2

    assert summary.providers == (
        "gemini",
        "openai",
    )


def test_failed_requests_are_counted() -> None:
    service = create_service()

    service.record(
        create_event(
            success=False,
        )
    )

    summary = service.summary()

    assert summary.failed_requests == 1

    assert summary.successful_requests == 0


def test_empty_service_returns_empty_summary() -> None:
    service = create_service()

    summary = service.summary()

    assert summary.total_requests == 0

    assert summary.total_tokens == 0

    assert summary.total_cost == Decimal(
        "0"
    )


def test_clear_removes_repository_data() -> None:
    service = create_service()

    service.record(
        create_event()
    )

    service.clear()

    summary = service.summary()

    assert summary.total_requests == 0

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\runtime\test_conversation_agent_provider.py
------------------------------------------------------------
"""
Tests for ConversationAgent LLM provider integration.

These tests verify the architecture:

ConversationAgent
    |
    v
LLMService
    |
    v
Injected LLMProvider
    |
    v
ProviderResult

No real provider API is called.
"""

import pytest

from backend.agents.conversation_agent import (
    ConversationAgent,
)

from backend.characters.character_profile import (
    CharacterProfile,
)

from backend.providers.provider_result import (
    ProviderResult,
)

from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import (
    ExecutionContext,
)


class FakeLLMProvider:
    """
    Deterministic provider for ConversationAgent tests.

    The provider is injected into ConversationAgent and
    therefore wrapped by LLMService internally.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        result: ProviderResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.received_request = None

    async def generate(
        self,
        request,
    ) -> ProviderResult:
        """
        Capture the request and return the configured result.
        """

        self.received_request = request

        return self.result


def create_character() -> CharacterProfile:
    """
    Create the deterministic test character.
    """

    return CharacterProfile(
        character_id="yeti",
        name="Yeti",
        description=(
            "Friendly AI learning companion"
        ),
        personality=(
            "Curious and encouraging"
        ),
        learning_style=(
            "Conversation based learning"
        ),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_executes_injected_provider():
    """
    ConversationAgent should execute an injected provider
    through the LLMService boundary.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output=(
                "Artificial intelligence is "
                "the field of building systems "
                "that perform tasks requiring intelligence."
            ),
        )
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_provider=provider,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain artificial intelligence",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert result.output["message"] == (
        "Artificial intelligence is "
        "the field of building systems "
        "that perform tasks requiring intelligence."
    )

    assert result.output["provider_id"] == (
        "fake_llm"
    )

    assert provider.received_request is not None

    assert (
        provider.received_request.user_prompt
        == "Explain artificial intelligence"
    )

    assert (
        provider.received_request.character_id
        == "yeti"
    )

    assert (
        agent.llm_service is not None
    )

    assert (
        agent.llm_service.llm_provider
        is provider
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_handles_provider_failure():
    """
    Provider failures should be converted into a failed
    AgentResult without exposing provider SDK details.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=False,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            error="Provider unavailable",
        )
    )

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character(),
        llm_provider=provider,
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain artificial intelligence",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is False

    assert result.output["provider_id"] == (
        "fake_llm"
    )

    assert result.output["provider_error"] == (
        "Provider unavailable"
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_conversation_agent_without_provider_keeps_demo_behaviour():
    """
    ConversationAgent should preserve deterministic
    hackathon behaviour when no LLM service/provider
    is configured.
    """

    agent = ConversationAgent(
        character=create_character()
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="demo-user",
        request="Explain artificial intelligence",
    )

    result = await agent.execute(
        context
    )

    assert result.success is True

    assert (
        result.output["message"]
        == (
            "Yeti: Let's explore this together. "
            "You asked: Explain artificial intelligence"
        )
    )

    assert (
        "provider_id"
        not in result.output
    )

    assert (
        agent.llm_service
        is None
    )


def test_conversation_agent_rejects_both_service_and_provider():
    """
    ConversationAgent should reject ambiguous dependency
    injection when both LLMService and LLMProvider are supplied.
    """

    provider = FakeLLMProvider(
        ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id="fake_llm",
            output="Test response",
        )
    )

    from backend.services.llm_service import (
        LLMService,
    )

    service = LLMService(
        provider
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match=(
            "Provide either llm_service or "
            "llm_provider, not both"
        ),
    ):
        ConversationAgent(
            character=create_character(),
            llm_service=service,
            llm_provider=provider,
        )

FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\tests\runtime\test_provider_contracts.py
------------------------------------------------------------
import pytest

from backend.providers.base_provider import BaseProvider
from backend.providers.provider_result import ProviderResult
from backend.runtime.context.execution_context import ExecutionContext


class DemoProvider(BaseProvider):

    async def execute(
        self,
        context: ExecutionContext,
    ) -> ProviderResult:

        return ProviderResult(
            success=True,
            provider_id=self.provider_id,
            output="Provider response",
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_provider_execution():

    provider = DemoProvider(
        provider_id="demo_provider"
    )

    context = ExecutionContext(
        user_id="user1",
        request="Generate response"
    )

    result = await provider.execute(context)

    assert result.success is True
    assert result.provider_id == "demo_provider"
    assert result.output == "Provider response"
